### [1] [A Synthesizable RTL Implementation of Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18066)
*Timothy Oh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了一种直接在硬件中实现预测编码学习动力学的完整可综合数字架构，通过局部预测误差动态实现分布式在线学习，避免了反向传播的全局依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播虽然推动了现代深度学习发展，但难以实现为完全分布式的在线硬件学习系统，因为它存在全局误差传播、相位分离和严重依赖集中式内存等问题。预测编码提供了一种替代方案，通过相邻层之间的局部预测误差动态实现推理和学习。

Method: 设计了一个确定性、可综合的RTL架构，围绕顺序MAC数据路径和固定有限状态调度构建。每个神经核心维护自身的活动、预测误差和突触权重，仅通过硬连线连接与相邻层通信。通过统一的每神经元钳制原语支持监督学习和推理，该原语在保持内部更新计划不变的情况下强制执行边界条件。

Result: 该工作贡献的不是新的学习规则，而是一个完整的可综合数字基板，直接在硬件中执行预测编码学习动态。系统在固定的局部更新规则下演化，通过连接性、参数和边界条件施加任务结构。

Conclusion: 该架构为预测编码学习动态提供了硬件实现方案，克服了反向传播在硬件实现中的局限性，支持完全分布式、在线的深度学习系统实现。

Abstract: Backpropagation has enabled modern deep learning but is difficult to realize as an online, fully distributed hardware learning system due to global error propagation, phase separation, and heavy reliance on centralized memory. Predictive coding offers an alternative in which inference and learning arise from local prediction-error dynamics between adjacent layers. This paper presents a digital architecture that implements a discrete-time predictive coding update directly in hardware. Each neural core maintains its own activity, prediction error, and synaptic weights, and communicates only with adjacent layers through hardwired connections. Supervised learning and inference are supported via a uniform per-neuron clamping primitive that enforces boundary conditions while leaving the internal update schedule unchanged. The design is a deterministic, synthesizable RTL substrate built around a sequential MAC datapath and a fixed finite-state schedule. Rather than executing a task-specific instruction sequence inside the learning substrate, the system evolves under fixed local update rules, with task structure imposed through connectivity, parameters, and boundary conditions. The contribution of this work is not a new learning rule, but a complete synthesizable digital substrate that executes predictive-coding learning dynamics directly in hardware.

</details>


### [2] [Constrained Hybrid Metaheuristic: A Universal Framework for Continuous Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18295)
*Piotr A. Kowalski,Szymon Kucharczyk,Jacek Mańdziuk*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: cHM算法是一个通用的连续优化框架，能够在广泛的函数类型上工作，包括具有未知、异构或复杂特性的函数，通过动态适应问题特征和利用候选解与元启发式策略之间的协同作用来提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元启发式算法通常针对特定函数类别或问题领域设计，缺乏一个能够处理具有未知、异构或复杂特性（如非凸性、不可分性和变化平滑度）的广泛目标函数的通用框架。

Method: 提出约束混合元启发式(cHM)算法，采用模块化结构和两阶段操作，能够动态适应问题特征。关键特点是能够利用候选解和组件元启发式策略之间的协同作用，在优化过程的每个阶段应用最合适的搜索行为。

Result: 在28个基准函数上的实验评估表明，cHM在解质量和收敛速度方面始终匹配或优于传统元启发式算法。在数据分类的特征选择问题中的实际应用也验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: cHM算法作为一个通用黑盒优化器，在理论和实际应用中都具有潜力，能够处理各种复杂特性的函数，并通过动态适应和协同机制提高优化性能。

Abstract: This paper presents the constrained Hybrid Metaheuristic (cHM) algorithm as a general framework for continuous optimisation. Unlike many existing metaheuristics that are tailored to specific function classes or problem domains, cHM is designed to operate across a broad spectrum of objective functions, including those with unknown, heterogeneous, or complex properties such as non-convexity, non-separability, and varying smoothness. We provide a formal description of the algorithm, highlighting its modular structure and two-phase operation, which facilitates dynamic adaptation to the problem's characteristics. A key feature of cHM is its ability to harness synergy between both candidate solutions and component metaheuristic strategies. This property allows the algorithm to apply the most appropriate search behaviour at each stage of the optimisation process, thereby improving convergence and robustness. Our extensive experimental evaluation on 28 benchmark functions demonstrates that cHM consistently matches or outperforms traditional metaheuristics in terms of solution quality and convergence speed. In addition, a practical application of the algorithm is demonstrated for a feature selection problem in the context of data classification. The results underscore its potential as a versatile and effective black-box optimiser suitable for both theoretical research and practical applications.

</details>

### [3] [TLGLock: A New Approach in Logic Locking Using Key-Driven Charge Recycling in Threshold Logic Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17809)
*Abdullah Sahruri,Martin Margala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TLGLock是一种新型逻辑锁定技术，利用阈值逻辑门和电荷回收实现高效硬件安全防护，相比传统方法在面积、延迟、功耗和SAT攻击抗性方面均有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前逻辑锁定技术在可扩展性和设计开销方面面临挑战，需要一种更高效、更安全的硬件防伪解决方案。

Method: 提出TLGLock设计范式，利用阈值逻辑门的结构表达能力和电荷回收的能量效率，在门级实现密钥依赖功能。通过将密钥嵌入门的加权逻辑并利用动态电荷共享，提供无状态且紧凑的锁定方案。

Result: 与锁存器基锁定方案相比，TLGLock实现高达30%面积节省、50%延迟降低和20%功耗节省；与XOR和SFLL-HD方法相比，提供高达3倍的SAT攻击抗性且开销显著更低；随机密钥权重实验显示错误密钥下可达100%输出损坏。

Conclusion: TLGLock作为一种可扩展且具有弹性的解决方案，为安全硬件设计提供了新的设计范式，在安全性和效率方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Logic locking remains one of the most promising defenses against hardware piracy, yet current approaches often face challenges in scalability and design overhead. In this paper, we present TLGLock, a new design paradigm that leverages the structural expressiveness of Threshold Logic Gates (TLGs) and the energy efficiency of charge recycling to enforce key-dependent functionality at the gate level. By embedding the key into the gate's weighted logic and utilizing dynamic charge sharing, TLGLock provides a stateless and compact alternative to conventional locking techniques. We implement a complete synthesis-to-locking flow and evaluate it using ISCAS, ITC, and MCNC benchmarks. Results show that TLGLock achieves up to 30% area, 50% delay, and 20% power savings compared to latch-based locking schemes. In comparison with XOR and SFLL-HD methods, TLGLock offers up to 3x higher SAT attack resistance with significantly lower overhead. Furthermore, randomized key-weight experiments demonstrate that TLGLock can reach up to 100% output corruption under incorrect keys, enabling tunable security at minimal cost. These results position TLGLock as a scalable and resilient solution for secure hardware design.

</details>


### [4] [PlanTwin: Privacy-Preserving Planning Abstractions for Cloud-Assisted LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18377)
*Guangsheng Yu,Qin Wang,Rui Lang,Shuai Su,Xu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PlanTwin：一种隐私保护架构，通过数字孪生技术在不暴露原始本地环境的情况下实现云辅助规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云托管的LLM已成为智能体系统中的规划器，但在许多部署中，规划环境包含源代码、文件、凭证等敏感信息，不能暴露给云端。现有解决方案主要关注执行隔离、访问控制或机密推理，但无法控制云规划器在规划过程中观察到的原始环境状态。

Method: 提出PlanTwin架构，将真实环境投影到规划导向的数字孪生中：一个模式约束和去标识化的抽象图，保留规划相关结构同时移除可重构细节。云规划器仅通过有界能力接口在此净化后的孪生体上操作，本地网关强制执行安全策略和累积披露预算。形式化隐私-效用权衡为能力粒度问题，使用(k,δ)-匿名性和ε-不可链接性定义隐私目标，并通过多轮披露控制缓解组合泄漏。

Result: 在10个领域的60个智能体任务上使用4个云规划器进行评估。PlanTwin实现了完全敏感项非披露(SND = 1.0)，同时保持接近全上下文系统的规划质量：4个规划器中的3个达到PQS > 0.79，完整流水线的效用损失小于2.2%。

Conclusion: PlanTwin提供了一种有效的隐私保护架构，能够在保护敏感本地环境信息的同时，维持云辅助规划的高质量执行，解决了现有方案中原始环境状态暴露的问题。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted large language models (LLMs) have become the de facto planners in agentic systems, coordinating tools and guiding execution over local environments. In many deployments, however, the environment being planned over is private, containing source code, files, credentials, and metadata that cannot be exposed to the cloud. Existing solutions address adjacent concerns, such as execution isolation, access control, or confidential inference, but they do not control what cloud planners observe during planning: within the permitted scope, \textit{raw environment state is still exposed}.
  We introduce PlanTwin, a privacy-preserving architecture for cloud-assisted planning without exposing raw local context. The key idea is to project the real environment into a \textit{planning-oriented digital twin}: a schema-constrained and de-identified abstract graph that preserves planning-relevant structure while removing reconstructable details. The cloud planner operates solely on this sanitized twin through a bounded capability interface, while a local gatekeeper enforces safety policies and cumulative disclosure budgets. We further formalize the privacy-utility trade-off as a capability granularity problem, define architectural privacy goals using $(k,δ)$-anonymity and $ε$-unlinkability, and mitigate compositional leakage through multi-turn disclosure control.
  We implement PlanTwin as middleware between local agents and cloud planners and evaluate it on 60 agentic tasks across ten domains with four cloud planners. PlanTwin achieves full sensitive-item non-disclosure (SND = 1.0) while maintaining planning quality close to full-context systems: three of four planners achieve PQS $> 0.79$, and the full pipeline incurs less than 2.2\% utility loss.

</details>


### [5] [Semantic Chameleon: Corpus-Dependent Poisoning Attacks and Defenses in RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18034)
*Scott Thornton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了针对检索增强生成（RAG）系统的梯度引导语料库投毒攻击，发现纯向量检索易受攻击（38%成功率），而混合检索（BM25+向量）能有效防御（降至0%）。攻击在Security Stack Exchange语料上有效，但在FEVER Wikipedia上无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通过外部知识源扩展LLM能力，但检索管道引入了新的攻击面。攻击者可以投毒检索语料库，使恶意文档在推理时被优先检索，从而操纵模型输出。需要研究此类攻击并评估无需修改底层LLM的检索层防御方法。

Method: 1. 实施双文档投毒攻击：包含睡眠文档和触发文档，使用贪婪坐标梯度（GCG）进行优化；2. 在Security Stack Exchange语料库（67,941文档）上进行大规模评估（50次攻击尝试）；3. 评估混合检索防御：结合BM25和向量相似度；4. 在五个LLM家族（GPT-5.3、GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Llama 4、GPT-4o-mini）上评估攻击成功率；5. 在FEVER Wikipedia数据集上进行跨语料评估（25次攻击）。

Result: 1. 梯度引导投毒在纯向量检索下达到38.0%的共检索率；2. 混合检索将攻击成功率从38%降至0%（无需修改模型或重新训练检索器）；3. 当攻击者同时优化稀疏和密集检索信号时，混合检索可被部分规避（20-44%成功率），但仍显著提高攻击难度；4. 在五个LLM家族上攻击成功率范围为46.7%至93.3%；5. FEVER Wikipedia数据集上所有检索配置的攻击成功率均为0%。

Conclusion: 梯度引导投毒攻击对纯向量检索的RAG系统构成严重威胁，但简单的混合检索架构修改能有效防御。攻击效果具有语料库依赖性，在Security Stack Exchange上有效而在FEVER Wikipedia上无效。研究强调了RAG系统检索层安全的重要性，并提供了实用的防御方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems extend large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge sources but introduce new attack surfaces through the retrieval pipeline. In particular, adversaries can poison retrieval corpora so that malicious documents are preferentially retrieved at inference time, enabling targeted manipulation of model outputs. We study gradient-guided corpus poisoning attacks against modern RAG pipelines and evaluate retrieval-layer defenses that require no modification to the underlying LLM.
  We implement dual-document poisoning attacks consisting of a sleeper document and a trigger document optimized using Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG). In a large-scale evaluation on the Security Stack Exchange corpus (67,941 documents) with 50 attack attempts, gradient-guided poisoning achieves a 38.0 percent co-retrieval rate under pure vector retrieval.
  We show that a simple architectural modification, hybrid retrieval combining BM25 and vector similarity, substantially mitigates this attack. Across all 50 attacks, hybrid retrieval reduces gradient-guided attack success from 38 percent to 0 percent without modifying the model or retraining the retriever. When attackers jointly optimize payloads for both sparse and dense retrieval signals, hybrid retrieval can be partially circumvented, achieving 20-44 percent success, but still significantly raises attack difficulty relative to vector-only retrieval.
  Evaluation across five LLM families (GPT-5.3, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Llama 4, and GPT-4o-mini) shows attack success ranging from 46.7 percent to 93.3 percent. Cross-corpus evaluation on the FEVER Wikipedia dataset (25 attacks) yields 0 percent attack success across all retrieval configurations.

</details>


### [6] [Confidential Databases Without Cryptographic Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18836)
*Wenxuan Huang,Zhanbo Wang,Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FEDB是一种新型机密数据库设计，通过将密码学操作移出关键路径，使用无密码映射来大幅降低性能开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密数据库在不可信云环境中保护敏感数据查询，但现有方案因频繁的同步密码学操作导致高性能开销，阻碍了广泛部署

Method: 采用无密码映射方法，在数据库中维护数据无关标识符，同时在可信域中安全映射到明文秘密，将密码学操作移出关键路径

Result: 在TPC-C和TPC-H等行业标准基准测试中，运行时开销降低了高达78.0倍

Conclusion: FEDB通过范式转变显著降低了机密数据库的性能开销，有望促进其在云环境中的广泛部署

Abstract: Confidential databases (CDBs) are essential for enabling secure queries over sensitive data in untrusted cloud environments using confidential computing hardware. While adoption is growing, widespread deployment is hindered by high performance overhead from frequent synchronous cryptographic operations, which causes significant computational and memory bottlenecks. We present FEDB, a novel CDB design that removes cryptographic operations from the critical path. FEDB leverages crypto-free mappings, which maintain data-independent identifiers within the database while securely mapping them to plaintext secrets in a trusted domain. This paradigm shift reduces the runtime overhead by up to 78.0 times on industry-standard benchmarks including TPC-C and TPC-H.

</details>


### [7] [Guardrails as Infrastructure: Policy-First Control for Tool-Orchestrated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18059)
*Akshey Sigdel,Rista Baral*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Policy-First Tooling框架，通过模型无关的权限层解决工具使用自动化系统的常见故障模式，包括不安全副作用、无效参数、失控重试和敏感信息泄漏等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用自动化系统（从脚本、CI机器人到智能助手）存在重复出现的故障模式，包括不安全副作用、无效参数、失控重试和敏感输出泄漏。现有缓解措施多为模型中心化和提示依赖型，因此脆弱且无法泛化到非LLM调用者。

Method: 提出Policy-First Tooling框架：1) 紧凑的策略领域特定语言（DSL）；2) 运行时强制执行架构，具有可操作的原理说明和修复提示；3) 基于轨迹重放的可复现基准，包含受控故障和误用注入。

Result: 在225个受控运行中，跨越5个策略包和3个故障配置文件：更严格的策略包将违规预防从P0的0.000提高到P4的0.681，任务成功率从0.356下降到0.067；重试放大从3.774降至1.378；在注入秘密输出的情况下，泄漏召回率达到0.875。

Conclusion: Policy-First Tooling框架通过明确的约束、风险感知的门控、恢复控制和可审计的解释，为工具调用提供模型无关的权限层，使安全性与实用性之间的权衡变得明确且可测量。

Abstract: Tool-using automation systems, from scripts and CI bots to agentic assistants, fail in recurring patterns. Common failures include unsafe side effects, invalid arguments, uncontrolled retries, and leakage of sensitive outputs. Many mitigations are model-centric and prompt-dependent, so they are brittle and do not generalize to non-LLM callers. We present Policy-First Tooling, a model-agnostic permission layer that mediates tool invocation through explicit constraints, risk-aware gating, recovery controls, and auditable explanations. The paper contributes a compact policy DSL, a runtime enforcement architecture with actionable rationale and fix hints, and a reproducible benchmark based on trace replay with controlled fault and misuse injection. In 225 controlled runs across five policy packs and three fault profiles, stricter packs improve violation prevention from 0.000 in P0 to 0.681 in P4, while task success drops from 0.356 to 0.067. Retry amplification decreases from 3.774 in P0 to 1.378 in P4, and leakage recall reaches 0.875 under injected secret outputs. These results make safety to utility trade-offs explicit and measurable.

</details>


### [8] [STEP: Detecting Audio Backdoor Attacks via Stability-based Trigger Exposure Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18103)
*Kun Wang,Meng Chen,Junhao Wang,Yuli Wu,Li Lu,Chong Zhang,Peng Cheng,Jiaheng Zhang,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: STEP是一种基于稳定性的黑盒后门检测方法，通过分析后门触发器的双重异常特性（语义破坏扰动下的异常标签稳定性与语义保留扰动下的异常标签脆弱性）来检测语音模型中的后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习语音模型在安全关键应用中的广泛部署，后门攻击已成为严重威胁。现有推理时防御方法不适用于音频领域，因为它们要么依赖于在基于变换和语义触发器上失败的过度鲁棒性假设，要么依赖于图像或文本模态的特定属性。

Method: STEP是一种黑盒、无需重新训练的后门检测器，仅需硬标签访问。其核心思想是利用后门触发器的双重异常特性：1) 在语义破坏扰动下的异常标签稳定性；2) 在语义保留扰动下的异常标签脆弱性。方法包括：使用两个互补的扰动分支对每个测试样本进行分析，通过在一类异常检测器上对生成的稳定性特征进行评分，并通过无监督加权融合两个分数。

Result: 在七种后门攻击上的广泛实验表明，STEP平均AUROC达到97.92%，EER为4.54%，显著优于现有最先进的基线方法，并在模型架构、语音任务、开放集验证场景以及空中物理世界设置中表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: STEP是一种有效的黑盒后门检测方法，通过利用后门触发器的双重异常特性，在语音模型中实现了高精度的后门检测，并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of deep-learning-based speech models in security-critical applications, backdoor attacks have emerged as a serious threat: an adversary who poisons a small fraction of training data can implant a hidden trigger that controls the model's output while preserving normal behavior on clean inputs. Existing inference-time defenses are not well suited to the audio domain, as they either rely on trigger over-robustness assumptions that fail on transformation-based and semantic triggers, or depend on properties specific to image or text modalities. In this paper, we propose STEP (Stability-based Trigger Exposure Profiling), a black-box, retraining-free backdoor detector that operates under hard-label-only access. Its core idea is to exploit a characteristic dual anomaly of backdoor triggers: anomalous label stability under semantic-breaking perturbations, and anomalous label fragility under semantic-preserving perturbations. STEP profiles each test sample with two complementary perturbation branches that target these two properties respectively, scores the resulting stability features with one-class anomaly detectors trained on benign references, and fuses the two scores via unsupervised weighting. Extensive experiments across seven backdoor attacks show that STEP achieves an average AUROC of 97.92% and EER of 4.54%, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, and generalizes across model architectures, speech tasks, an open-set verification scenario, and over-the-air physical-world settings.

</details>


### [9] [Adaptive Fuzzy Logic-Based Steganographic Encryption Framework: A Comprehensive Experimental Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18105)
*Aadi Joshi,Kavya Bhand*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合模糊推理系统和认证加密的自适应隐写框架，通过局部熵、边缘幅度和载荷压力动态确定像素级嵌入深度（1-3比特），同时使用加密层保护载荷机密性和完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定深度LSB嵌入在平滑和纹理区域采用统一修改策略，导致失真增加和统计可检测性提升。需要一种自适应方法在保持高容量的同时，优化视觉保真度和统计不可检测性之间的权衡。

Method: 结合Mamdani型模糊推理系统和现代认证加密。使用局部熵、边缘幅度和载荷压力作为模糊输入，动态确定每个像素的嵌入深度（1-3比特）。通过从低位剥离图像计算特征图，使自适应控制机制对LSB修改保持不变。采用Argon2id和AES-256-GCM加密层独立保护载荷机密性和完整性。

Result: 该方法实现了像素级自适应嵌入深度控制，在保持高容量的同时，通过将修改集中在纹理丰富区域来减少视觉失真和统计可检测性。加密层提供了独立于隐写隐藏的载荷保护。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应隐写框架通过模糊推理系统优化了嵌入策略，结合现代加密技术提供了全面的安全保护，在容量、视觉保真度和统计不可检测性之间实现了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Digital image steganography requires a careful trade-off among payload capacity, visual fidelity, and statistical undetectability. Fixed-depth least significant bit embedding remains attractive because of its simplicity and high capacity, but it modifies smooth and textured regions uniformly, thereby increasing distortion and detectability in statistically sensitive areas. This paper presents an adaptive steganographic framework that combines a Mamdanitype fuzzy inference system with modern authenticated encryption. The proposed method determines a pixel-wise embedding depth from 1 to 3 bits using local entropy, edge magnitude, and payload pressure as linguistic inputs. To preserve encoder-decoder synchronization, the same feature maps are computed from lower-bit-stripped images, making the adaptive control mechanism invariant to the least significant modifications introduced during embedding. A cryptographic layer based on Argon2id and AES-256-GCM protects payload confidentiality and integrity independently of steganographic concealment.

</details>


### [10] [CNT: Safety-oriented Function Reuse across LLMs via Cross-Model Neuron Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18449)
*Yue Zhao,Yujia Gong,Ruigang Liang,Shenchen Zhu,Kai Chen,Xuejing Yuan,Wangjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CNT是一种后处理方法，通过从开源捐赠LLM向目标LLM转移神经元子集，实现安全功能的重用，支持功能添加和删除，在多种安全应用中表现优异且性能损失小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的广泛部署，需要灵活的后处理方法来适应不断变化的安全需求。开源LLM生态系统已经产生了具有各种安全功能的不同模型，这促使从零构建安全功能转向重用现有模型的功能，以避免昂贵的数据收集和训练过程。

Method: 提出跨模型神经元转移（CNT）方法，通过从开源捐赠LLM向目标LLM转移最小神经元子集来重用安全导向功能。该方法在神经元级别操作，支持模块化的功能级适应，包括功能添加和删除。

Result: 在七个流行LLM和三个代表性应用（安全失对齐、对齐增强和偏见消除）上的实验表明，CNT实现了有针对性的安全功能转移，性能退化最小（大多数模型小于1%），始终优于五个基线方法，证明了其通用性和实际有效性。

Conclusion: CNT提供了一种高效、模块化的后处理方法，通过重用现有模型的安全功能，避免了昂贵的训练过程，为LLM安全适应提供了一种实用解决方案。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of large language models (LLMs) calls for post-hoc methods that can flexibly adapt models to evolving safety requirements. Meanwhile, the rapidly expanding open-source LLM ecosystem has produced a diverse collection of models that already exhibit various safety-related functionalities. This motivates a shift from constructing safety functionality from scratch to reusing existing functionality from external models, thereby avoiding costly data collection and training procedures.
  In this paper, we present Cross-Model Neuron Transfer (CNT), a post-hoc method that reuses safety-oriented functionality by transferring a minimal subset of neurons from an open-source donor LLM to a target LLM. By operating at the neuron level, CNT enables modular function-level adaptation, supporting both function addition andfunction deletion. We evaluate CNT on seven popular LLMs across three representative applications: safety disalignment, alignment enhancement, and bias removal. Experimental results show that CNT achieves targeted safety-oriented functionality transfer with minimal performance degradation (less than 1% for most models), consistently outperforming five baselines, demonstrating its generality and practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [11] [Cross-Ecosystem Vulnerability Analysis for Python Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18693)
*Georgios Alexopoulos,Nikolaos Alexopoulos,Thodoris Sotiropoulos,Charalambos Mitropoulos,Zhendong Su,Dimitris Mitropoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种溯源感知的漏洞分析方法，通过内容哈希匹配和动态分析识别Python包中vendor的本地库版本，构建跨生态系统调用图进行漏洞可达性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python应用依赖的本地库可能被vendor在包分发中或安装在主机系统。当这些库发现漏洞时，确定哪些Python包受影响需要跨生态系统分析。现有漏洞扫描器因忽略vendor漏洞而产生假阴性，又因忽略操作系统发行版的安全补丁而产生假阳性。

Method: 1. 溯源感知漏洞分析方法：将vendor库解析到特定OS包版本或上游发布；2. 使用基于内容的哈希查询vendor库与历史OS包工件数据库；3. 应用库特定的动态分析从上游源码构建的二进制文件中提取版本信息；4. 通过跨依赖边界缝合Python和二进制调用图构建跨生态系统调用图，实现漏洞函数可达性分析。

Result: 在10万个Python包和10个已知CVE的评估中：1. 识别出39个直接易受攻击的包（每月下载量超过4700万）；2. 识别出312个通过依赖链间接易受攻击的客户端包；3. 与上游版本匹配相比，实现了高达97%的假阳性减少。

Conclusion: 该方法通过精确识别vendor库版本和构建跨生态系统调用图，显著提高了Python包中第三方本地依赖漏洞检测的准确性，减少了假阳性和假阴性。

Abstract: Python applications depend on native libraries that may be vendored within package distributions or installed on the host system. When vulnerabilities are discovered in these libraries, determining which Python packages are affected requires cross-ecosystem analysis spanning Python dependency graphs and OS package versions. Current vulnerability scanners produce false negatives by missing vendored vulnerabilities and false positives by ignoring security patches backported by OS distributions.
  We present a provenance-aware vulnerability analysis approach that resolves vendored libraries to specific OS package versions or upstream releases. Our approach queries vendored libraries against a database of historical OS package artifacts using content-based hashing, and applies library-specific dynamic analyses to extract version information from binaries built from upstream source. We then construct cross-ecosystem call graphs by stitching together Python and binary call graphs across dependency boundaries, enabling reachability analysis of vulnerable functions. Evaluating on 100,000 Python packages and 10 known CVEs associated with third-party native dependencies, we identify 39 directly vulnerable packages (47M+ monthly downloads) and 312 indirectly vulnerable client packages affected through dependency chains. Our analysis achieves up to 97% false positive reduction compared to upstream version matching.

</details>


### [12] [Pushan: Trace-Free Deobfuscation of Virtualization-Obfuscated Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18355)
*Ashwin Sudhir,Zion Leonahenahe Basque,Wil Gibbs,Ati Priya Bajaj,Pulkit Singh Singaria,Mitchell Zakocs,Jie Hu,Moritz Schloegel,Tiffany Bao,Adam Doupe,Yan Shoshitaishvili,Ruoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PUSHAN是一种新型的虚拟化混淆二进制反混淆技术，无需执行轨迹，避免路径约束积累，能够恢复完整的控制流图并生成高质量的C伪代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟化混淆反混淆技术存在三个主要缺陷：1) 仅适用于执行轨迹，无法恢复所有逻辑；2) 依赖昂贵的动态符号执行，可扩展性差；3) 无法生成"格式良好"的代码，导致反编译器无法生成人类友好的输出。

Method: PUSHAN采用无轨迹方法，通过VPC敏感、无约束的符号模拟来避免路径约束积累，恢复虚拟化函数的完整控制流图。这是首个能够将受保护代码反编译为高质量C伪代码的方法，并规避了路径可满足性这一NP难问题的依赖。

Result: 在超过1,000个二进制文件上评估PUSHAN，包括学术前沿(Tigress)和商业级混淆器(VMProtect和Themida)保护的目标。PUSHAN成功反混淆这些二进制文件，检索其完整控制流图，并将其反编译为C伪代码。进一步通过分析VirusTotal中先前未分析的VMProtect混淆恶意软件样本，展示了其适用性。

Conclusion: PUSHAN克服了现有虚拟化混淆反混淆技术的局限性，提供了一种可扩展、完整且能生成高质量反编译输出的通用方法，显著提升了恶意软件分析的有效性。

Abstract: In the ever-evolving battle against malware, binary obfuscation techniques are a formidable barrier to effective analysis by both human security analysts and automated systems. In particular, virtualization or VM-based obfuscation is one of the strongest protection mechanisms that evade automated analysis. Despite widespread use of virtualization, existing automated deobfuscation techniques suffer from three major drawbacks. First, they only work on execution traces, which prevents them from recovering all logic in an obfuscated binary. Second, they depend on dynamic symbolic execution, which is expensive and does not scale in practice. Third, they cannot generate "well-formed" code, which prevents existing binary decompilers from generating human-friendly output.
  This paper introduces PUSHAN, a novel and generic technique for deobfuscating virtualization-obfuscated binaries while overcoming the limitations of existing techniques. PUSHAN is trace-free and avoids path-constraint accumulation by using VPC-sensitive, constraint-free symbolic emulation to recover a complete CFG of the virtualized function. It is the first approach that also decompiles the protected code into high-quality C pseudocode to enable effective analysis. Crucially, PUSHAN circumvents reliance on path satisfiability, a known NP-hard problem that hampers scalability. We evaluate PUSHAN on more than 1,000 binaries, including targets protected by academic state of the art (Tigress) and commercial-strength obfuscators VMProtect and Themida. PUSHAN successfully deobfuscates these binaries, retrieves their complete CFGs, and decompiles them to C pseudocode. We further demonstrate applicability by analyzing a previously unanalyzed VMProtect-obfuscated malware sample from VirusTotal, where our decompiled output enables LLM-assisted code simplification, reuse, and program understanding.

</details>


### [13] [Prompt Control-Flow Integrity: A Priority-Aware Runtime Defense Against Prompt Injection in LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18433)
*Md Takrim Ul Alam,Akif Islam,Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Abu Saleh Musa Miah,Jungpil Shin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PCFI是一种针对LLM API和RAG系统的运行时防御机制，通过建模提示的结构化组成和优先级感知策略执行来防御提示注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM API和RAG系统容易受到提示注入攻击，现有防御方法将提示视为扁平字符串，依赖临时过滤或静态越狱检测，缺乏结构化分析和优先级感知。

Method: 提出Prompt Control-Flow Integrity (PCFI)，将请求建模为系统、开发者、用户和检索文档段的结构化组合，采用三阶段中间件管道：词汇启发式、角色切换检测和分层策略执行。

Result: 在自定义基准测试中，PCFI拦截所有攻击标记请求，保持0%误报率，仅引入0.04毫秒的中位处理开销。

Conclusion: 基于来源和优先级感知的提示执行是部署LLM系统的实用轻量级防御方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed behind APIs and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) stacks are vulnerable to prompt injection attacks that may override system policies, subvert intended behavior, and induce unsafe outputs. Existing defenses often treat prompts as flat strings and rely on ad hoc filtering or static jailbreak detection. This paper proposes Prompt Control-Flow Integrity (PCFI), a priority-aware runtime defense that models each request as a structured composition of system, developer, user, and retrieved-document segments. PCFI applies a three-stage middleware pipeline, lexical heuristics, role-switch detection, and hierarchical policy enforcement, before forwarding requests to the backend LLM. We implement PCFI as a FastAPI-based gateway for deployed LLM APIs and evaluate it on a custom benchmark of synthetic and semi-realistic prompt-injection workloads. On the evaluated benchmark suite, PCFI intercepts all attack-labeled requests, maintains a 0% False Positive Rate, and introduces a median processing overhead of only 0.04 ms. These results suggest that provenance- and priority-aware prompt enforcement is a practical and lightweight defense for deployed LLM systems.

</details>


### [14] [Impact of Differentials in SIMON32 Algorithm for Lightweight Security of Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18455)
*Jonathan Cook,Sabih ur Rehman,M. Arif Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了SIMON32密码的差分特性，通过识别高概率差分提高了密码分析的效率，突破了现有技术基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIMON作为物联网设备的高效加密算法，其密码分析效率低下，主要原因是差分分布表规模大和高概率差分稀缺。需要改进密码分析效率以更好地评估SIMON的安全性。

Method: 对SIMON32密码进行差分特性分析，利用部分差分分布表识别高概率差分，揭示差分特征，为未来效率提升铺平道路。

Result: 分析发现了SIMON密码的高概率差分，增加了目标轮数，超过了现有技术基准。相关代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: 通过分析SIMON32的差分特性，识别高概率差分，提高了密码分析效率，为SIMON密码的安全性评估提供了更有效的方法。

Abstract: SIMON and SPECK were among the first efficient encryption algorithms introduced for resource-constrained applications. SIMON is suitable for Internet of Things (IoT) devices and has rapidly attracted the attention of the research community to understand its structure and analyse its security. To analyse the security of an encryption algorithm, researchers often employ cryptanalysis techniques. However, cryptanalysis is a resource and time-intensive task. To improve cryptanalysis efficiency, state-of-the-art research has proposed implementing heuristic search and sampling methods. Despite recent advances, the cryptanalysis of the SIMON cypher remains inefficient. Contributing factors are the large size of the difference distribution tables utilised in cryptanalysis and the scarcity of differentials with a high transition probability. To address these limitations, we introduce an analysis of differential properties of the SIMON32 cypher, revealing differential characteristics that pave the way for future efficiency enhancements. Our analysis has further increased the number of targeted rounds by identifying high probability differentials within a partial difference distribution table of the SIMON cypher, exceeding existing state-of-the-art benchmarks. The code designed for this work is available at https://github.com/johncook1979/simon32-analysis.

</details>


### [15] [Quantifying Memory Cells Vulnerability for DRAM Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18549)
*Zilong Hu,Hongming Fei,Prosanta Gope,Jack Miskelly,Owen Millwood,Biplab Sikdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个定量、单元级的电路框架，从物理电荷泄漏和干扰路径直接建模DRAM漏洞，将设备层行为与系统级安全属性关联，评估DRAM在易失性、完整性和机密性方面的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAM中的单元漏洞（强制保留失败、延迟改变、行锤击和行压力）会导致非预期的比特翻转，这些现象既被用作攻击手段破坏数据完整性和机密性，也被安全系统利用来生成随机密钥和设备指纹。攻击者可能利用对单个单元翻转漏洞的了解来预测数据内容，从而破坏安全系统。需要建立定量框架来评估DRAM在安全应用中的适用性。

Method: 开发了一个定量、单元级的电路框架，直接从DRAM的物理电荷泄漏和干扰路径建模其漏洞。该框架将设备层行为（电荷泄漏、干扰路径）与系统级安全属性（易失性、完整性、机密性）联系起来，实现了对DRAM安全性的系统评估。

Result: 该框架能够系统评估DRAM在易失性（保留）、完整性（干扰引起的修改）和机密性（模式依赖的泄漏）方面的安全性。应用于已知故障模式时，揭示了非均匀和上下文依赖的漏洞模式。

Conclusion: 这项工作为评估DRAM在安全应用中的适用性提供了理论基础和实际评估工具，通过将物理设备行为与系统安全属性联系起来，实现了对DRAM安全性的定量分析框架。

Abstract: Dynamic Random Access Memory (DRAM) is pervasive in computer systems. Cell vulnerabilities caused by unintended phenomena (forced retention failure, latency alteration, rowhammer and rowpress) lead to unintended bit flips in memory. These phenomena have been explored as attacks to violate data integrity and confidentiality during normal operation, but also exploited as a benefit in security systems as a method to generate random secret keys and unique device fingerprints (e.g. Physically Unclonable Functions). In both cases, attackers may wish to exploit knowledge of individual cell flip vulnerability to predict the current/future data contents of a set of cells, which can be utilised to break security systems. In this work, we develop a quantitative, cell-level circuit framework that models DRAM vulnerability directly from its physical charge leakage and disturbance pathways. By linking these device-layer behaviours to system-level security properties, our framework enables systematic evaluation of DRAM with respect to volatility (retention), integrity (disturbance-induced modification), and confidentiality (pattern-dependent leakage). We further demonstrate how the framework can be applied to well-known failure modes, revealing non-uniform and context-dependent vulnerability patterns. This work provides both theoretical foundations and practical evaluation tools for evaluating the suitability of DRAM use within security applications.

</details>


### [16] [A Complexity Hierarchy of Shuffles in Card-Based Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18608)
*Tomoki Ono,Suthee Ruangwises*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了基于卡牌密码学中洗牌操作的复杂度层次结构，并证明了不同层次间的分离结果，同时基于此层次结构提出了新的协议复杂度度量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于卡牌的密码协议使用各种类型的洗牌操作，其中一些在实践中易于实现，而另一些则复杂得多。需要对这些洗牌操作进行分类和层次化，以便从实践和理论角度理解其实现复杂度。

Method: 根据实现复杂度将洗牌操作分为多个层次，从实践和理论角度论证这一层次结构的合理性，通过证明某些洗牌操作无法仅使用低层次操作来实现，展示不同层次间的分离结果。

Result: 建立了洗牌操作的复杂度层次结构，证明了不同层次间的分离性，并基于此层次结构提出了评估卡牌协议的新复杂度度量方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于卡牌的密码学提供了洗牌操作的系统化分类框架，有助于更好地理解和评估协议的实际可行性，并为协议设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Card-based cryptography uses physical playing cards to construct protocols for secure multi-party computation. Existing card-based protocols employ various types of shuffles, some of which are easy to implement in practice while others are considerably more complex. In this paper, we classify shuffle operations into several levels according to their implementation complexity. We motivate this hierarchy from both practical and theoretical perspectives, and prove separation results between several levels by showing that certain shuffles cannot be realized using only operations from lower levels. Finally, we propose a new complexity measure for evaluating card-based protocols based on this hierarchy.

</details>


### [17] [MOSAIC: Multi-Objective Slice-Aware Iterative Curation for Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18637)
*Yipu Dou,Wang Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个多目标切片感知迭代优化框架，用于在固定监督微调预算下平衡安全对齐、低过度拒绝和指令跟随三个目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要解决在有限监督微调预算下同时平衡三个关键目标的问题：多轮安全对齐、良性边界查询的低过度拒绝、以及可验证约束下的指令跟随能力。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，基于统一的L1-L3评估接口构建闭环数据混合搜索，将切片级失败模式转化为可执行的数据操作，包括数据集级混合比例、桶级权重和聚焦标准。

Result: 在固定1M令牌预算和五轮独立微调下，MOSAIC将内部XGuard从2.76提升到4.67，同时保持OrBench在4.41和IFEval在3.65。最终Pareto解在独立攻击、过度拒绝和能力测试上比随机静态LoRA基线有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结构化失败诊断可以作为预算数据构建的实际控制信号，MOSAIC框架在有限预算下有效平衡了多个对齐目标。

Abstract: We study how to allocate a fixed supervised fine-tuning budget when three objectives must be balanced at once: multi-turn safety alignment, low over-refusal on benign boundary queries, and instruction following under verifiable constraints. We propose MOSAIC (Multi-Objective Slice-Aware Iterative Curation for Alignment), a multi-objective framework for closed-loop data mixture search built on a unified L1-L3 evaluation interface. MOSAIC turns slice-level failure profiles into executable data actions, including dataset-level mixture ratios, bucket-level weights, and focus criteria. Under a fixed 1M-token budget and five rounds of independent fine-tuning from the same base model, MOSAIC improves internal XGuard from 2.76 to 4.67 while keeping OrBench at 4.41 and IFEval at 3.65. The final Pareto solution also generalizes better than a random static LoRA baseline on independent attack, over-refusal, and capability tests, suggesting that structured failure diagnosis can serve as a practical control signal for budgeted data construction. Code is available at https://github.com/douyipu/mosaic.

</details>


### [18] [Secure Wi-Fi Ranging Today: Security and Adoption of IEEE 802.11az/bk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18687)
*Nikola Antonijević,Bernhard Etzlinger,Dave Singelée,Bart Preneel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文分析Wi-Fi安全测距标准IEEE 802.11az和802.11bk的安全性和可部署性，揭示配置敏感性和硬件实现困难


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着测距和定位技术对关键应用日益重要，Wi-Fi作为广泛部署的技术自然成为提供这些功能的候选方案。IEEE 802.11az引入了"下一代定位"机制来增强现有不安全的Wi-Fi精细时间测量测距方案，而802.11bk则通过增加带宽来提高精度。然而，这些标准承诺的安全性和可部署性需要从实际角度进行评估。

Method: 结合标准分析、仿真和商用/开发硬件的测量，从逻辑层和物理层分析安全Wi-Fi测距的核心机制。逻辑层分析部署选择如何导致未认证测距、降级攻击和拒绝服务攻击；物理层研究安全测距波形的可预测性、符号重复的安全影响，以及波形设计选择在现实射频行为下对频谱掩模合规性的影响。

Result: 研究表明安全Wi-Fi测距对配置选择高度敏感，在现有硬件上实现非易事。常见部署选择可能导致未认证测距、降级攻击和简单拒绝服务攻击，难以安全实现高风险用例。物理层分析显示波形可预测性和符号重复存在安全隐患。目前商用设备对安全Wi-Fi测距的支持有限。

Conclusion: 论文提供了安全使用精细时间测量的实用指南，并向供应商和标准化机构提出了改进其鲁棒性和可部署性的建议。安全Wi-Fi测距的实现需要仔细考虑配置选择和硬件限制，标准化工作仍需进一步完善以确保实际部署的安全性。

Abstract: Ranging and localisation have become critical for many applications and services. The Wi-Fi (IEEE 802.11) standard is a natural candidate for providing these functions across diverse environments, given its widespread deployment. The IEEE 802.11az amendment, finalised in 2023, introduces "Next Generation Positioning" mechanisms to secure and harden the existing insecure Wi-Fi Fine Timing Measurement (FTM) ranging solution. Moreover, the recent IEEE 802.11bk amendment increases the available bandwidth with the goal of approaching the centimetre-level ranging accuracy of ultra-wideband (UWB) systems. This paper examines to what extent these promises hold from a security and deployability perspective. We analyse the core mechanisms of secure Wi-Fi ranging as defined in IEEE 802.11az and IEEE 802.11bk at both the logical and physical layers, combining standards analysis with simulations and measurements on commercial and development hardware. At the logical layer, we show how common deployment choices can result in unauthenticated ranging, downgrade attacks, and simple denial-of-service attacks, making it difficult to securely realise many high-stakes use cases. At the physical layer, we study the predictability of secure ranging waveforms, the security impact of symbol repetition, and how waveform design choices affect compliance with spectral masks under realistic RF behaviour. Our results show that secure Wi-Fi ranging is highly sensitive to configuration choices and is non-trivial to implement on existing hardware. This is also evidenced by the currently limited support for secure Wi-Fi ranging in commodity devices. This paper provides practical guidelines for using secure FTM safely and recommendations to vendors and standardisation bodies to improve its robustness and deployability.

</details>


### [19] [Functional Subspace Watermarking for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18793)
*Zikang Ding,Junhao Li,Suling Wu,Junchi Yao,Hongbo Liu,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FSW是一种针对大语言模型的功能子空间水印框架，通过将所有权信号锚定在低维功能骨干中，在保持模型效用的同时提升对参数级扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型水印方法在应对实际模型修改（如微调、量化、知识蒸馏）时，内部特征会经历复杂扭曲，导致可靠提取极其困难，缺乏对参数级扰动的足够鲁棒性。

Method: 提出功能子空间水印（FSW）框架：1）通过求解广义特征值问题提取稳定的功能子空间用于水印注入；2）引入自适应谱截断策略在鲁棒性和模型效用间实现最优平衡；3）加入向量一致性约束确保水印注入不损害原始语义性能。

Result: 在各种LLM架构和数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在多种模型攻击下实现了优越的检测准确率和统计可验证性，其鲁棒性优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: FSW通过将所有权信号锚定在低维功能骨干中，有效解决了现有模型水印方法对参数级扰动鲁棒性不足的问题，为LLM所有权保护提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Model watermarking utilizes internal representations to protect the ownership of large language models (LLMs). However, these features inevitably undergo complex distortions during realistic model modifications such as fine-tuning, quantization, or knowledge distillation, making reliable extraction extremely challenging. Despite extensive research on model-side watermarking, existing methods still lack sufficient robustness against parameter-level perturbations. To address this gap, we propose \texttt{\textbf{Functional Subspace Watermarking (FSW)}}, a framework that anchors ownership signals into a low-dimensional functional backbone. Specifically, we first solve a generalized eigenvalue problem to extract a stable functional subspace for watermark injection, while introducing an adaptive spectral truncation strategy to achieve an optimal balance between robustness and model utility. Furthermore, a vector consistency constraint is incorporated to ensure that watermark injection does not compromise the original semantic performance. Extensive experiments across various LLM architectures and datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior detection accuracy and statistical verifiability under multiple model attacks, maintaining robustness that outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods.

</details>


### [20] [Agent Control Protocol: Admission Control for Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18829)
*Marcelo Fernandez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Agent Control Protocol (ACP) 是一个用于B2B机构环境中自主智能体治理的正式技术规范，作为智能体意图与系统状态变更之间的准入控制层，通过加密验证确保身份、能力范围、委托链和政策合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在B2B机构环境中，需要确保自主智能体的操作受到明确的机构控制，防止未经授权的系统状态变更，同时保持与现有安全框架（如RBAC和零信任）的兼容性。

Method: ACP定义了加密身份、基于能力的授权、确定性风险评估、可验证链式委托、传递式撤销和不可变审计等机制。它作为RBAC和零信任之上的附加层运行，包含36个技术文档、Go参考实现、51个签名一致性测试向量和OpenAPI 3.1.0规范。

Result: v1.13规范包含5个一致性级别（L1-L5），22个Go包覆盖L1-L4能力，定义了超过62个可验证要求、12个禁止行为，以及机构间互操作性机制。

Conclusion: ACP为自主智能体在机构环境中的治理提供了完整的技术框架，通过加密准入控制确保安全性和合规性，同时保持与现有安全架构的兼容性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Agent Control Protocol (ACP) is a formal technical specification for governance of autonomous agents in B2B institutional environments. ACP is the admission control layer between agent intent and system state mutation: before any agent action reaches execution, it must pass a cryptographic admission check that validates identity, capability scope, delegation chain, and policy compliance simultaneously.
  ACP defines the mechanisms of cryptographic identity, capability-based authorization, deterministic risk evaluation, verifiable chained delegation, transitive revocation, and immutable auditing that a system must implement for autonomous agents to operate under explicit institutional control. ACP operates as an additional layer on top of RBAC and Zero Trust, without replacing them.
  The v1.13 specification comprises 36 technical documents organized into five conformance levels (L1-L5). It includes a Go reference implementation of 22 packages covering all L1-L4 capabilities, 51 signed conformance test vectors (Ed25519 + SHA-256), and an OpenAPI 3.1.0 specification for all HTTP endpoints. It defines more than 62 verifiable requirements, 12 prohibited behaviors, and the mechanisms for interoperability between institutions.
  Specification and implementation: https://github.com/chelof100/acp-framework-en

</details>


### [21] [Security, privacy, and agentic AI in a regulatory view: From definitions and distinctions to provisions and reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18914)
*Shiliang Zhang,Sabita Maharjan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了2024-2025年欧盟AI监管框架，聚焦自主AI（agentic AI）在安全与隐私领域的监管挑战，通过24份政策文件梳理澄清关键定义，解构监管解释，并提出改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术的快速发展和自主AI的兴起使得传统监管框架难以跟上技术步伐，特别是在安全与隐私领域，自主AI的能力模糊了法律与技术边界，需要系统分析现有监管规定以解决这一挑战。

Method: 通过分析2024-2025年间发布的24份欧盟AI监管相关文件，采用文献综述方法，澄清关键定义，解构安全、隐私和自主AI的监管解释，区分相关概念，并综合评估针对不同类型AI的监管规定。

Result: 研究明确了欧盟AI监管框架中关键概念的定义，解构了安全、隐私和自主AI的监管解释，识别了现有规定与AI技术特性之间的差距，特别是自主AI行为带来的监管挑战。

Conclusion: 需要调整安全与隐私义务以更好地适应AI和自主行为特性，为政策制定者、开发者和研究者提供合规与AI治理的见解，以应对日益增长的算法自主性社会。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of artificial intelligence (AI) technologies has led to a dynamic regulatory landscape, where legislative frameworks strive to keep pace with technical advancements. As AI paradigms shift towards greater autonomy, specifically in the form of agentic AI, it becomes increasingly challenging to precisely articulate regulatory stipulations. This challenge is even more acute in the domains of security and privacy, where the capabilities of autonomous agents often blur traditional legal and technical boundaries. This paper reviews the evolving European Union (EU) AI regulatory provisions via analyzing 24 relevant documents published between 2024 and 2025. From this review, we provide a clarification of critical definitions. We deconstruct the regulatory interpretations of security, privacy, and agentic AI, distinguishing them from closely related concepts to resolve ambiguity. We synthesize the reviewed documents to articulate the current state of regulatory provisions targeting different types of AI, particularly those related to security and privacy aspects. We analyze and reflect on the existing provisions in the regulatory dimension to better align security and privacy obligations with AI and agentic behaviors. These insights serve to inform policymakers, developers, and researchers on the compliance and AI governance in the society with increasing algorithmic agencies.

</details>


### [22] [Controller Datapath Aware Verification of Masked Hardware Generated via High Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18939)
*Nilotpola Sarma,Vaishali Ghanshyam Chaudhuri,Chandan Karfa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出针对HLS生成掩码硬件的验证工具MaskedHLSVerif，解决现有工具在资源共享数据路径架构下产生假阳性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLS工具自动生成掩码RTL硬件可减少设计工作量，但HLS优化可能影响PSCA安全性，而现有硬件掩码验证工具在HLS生成的控制器数据路径架构中会产生假阳性

Method: 提出MaskedHLSVerif工具流，对HLS生成的控制器数据路径RTL进行状态级形式验证，避免因资源共享数据路径导致的假阳性

Result: 工具正确验证了级联掩码模块和PRESENT S-box掩码实现，而现有工具如REBECCA报告假阳性；同时能检测HLS优化引入的掩码缺陷

Conclusion: MaskedHLSVerif为HLS生成的掩码硬件提供了有效的验证策略，解决了现有工具在资源共享架构下的假阳性问题，并能检测HLS优化引入的安全缺陷

Abstract: Masking is a countermeasure against Power Side Channel Attacks (PSCAs) in both software and hardware implementations of cryptographic algorithms. Compared to software masking, implementing masked hardware is time consuming and error prone. Recent approaches, therefore, rely on High Level Synthesis (HLS) tools to automatically generate masked Register Transfer Level (RTL) hardware from verified masked software, significantly reducing design effort. Since HLS was never developed for security, HLS optimizations may impact PSCA security of the generated RTL. As a result, verifying the PSCA security of HLS generated masked RTL is crucial. Existing hardware masking verification tools can verify masked hardware, but may produce false positives when applied to HLS generated designs with controller datapath architectures obtained due to resource-shared datapath obtained via HLS. This work proposes a hardware masking verification strategy for HLS generated masked hardware. Our toolflow MaskedHLSVerif, performs state-wise formal verification of controller datapath RTL obtained via HLS, thereby avoiding false positives caused by resource-shared datapaths. Our tool flow correctly verifies standard cryptographic benchmarks, consisting of cascaded masked gadgets and the PRESENT S-box masked with gadgets, where existing tools like REBECCA reports false positives. The proposed tool-flow is able to detect masking flaws induced by HLS-optimizations as well.

</details>


### [23] [FedTrident: Resilient Road Condition Classification Against Poisoning Attacks in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19101)
*Sheng Liu,Panos Papadimitratos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedTrident是一个针对联邦学习道路状况分类中目标标签翻转攻击的防御框架，通过神经元分析检测恶意本地模型、自适应客户端评级进行排除，以及机器遗忘修复已损坏的全局模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在智能交通系统（特别是基于摄像头的道路状况分类）中具有重要应用，但面临目标标签翻转攻击的威胁。现有防御措施在多种攻击场景下无法维持接近无攻击水平的模型性能，主要因为：1）未针对TLFAs定制本地模型检测；2）未基于历史行为排除恶意车辆客户端；3）排除恶意客户端后未修复已损坏的全局模型。

Method: FedTrident包含三个核心组件：1）基于神经元分析的本地模型异常检测（包括攻击目标识别、关键特征提取、GMM聚类和过滤）；2）根据每轮FL的检测结果进行自适应客户端评级以排除恶意客户端；3）在FL过程中排除恶意客户端后，使用机器遗忘技术修复已损坏的全局模型。

Result: 在多种FL-RCC模型、任务和配置下的广泛评估表明，FedTrident能有效抵御TLFAs，在无攻击场景下达到可比较的性能，在两个最关键指标上分别优于八个基线防御方法9.49%和4.47%。此外，FedTrident对各种恶意客户端比例、数据异构性水平、复杂多任务和动态攻击都具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedTrident填补了联邦学习道路状况分类中对抗目标标签翻转攻击的研究空白，通过综合的检测、排除和修复机制，为智能交通系统提供了安全可靠的联邦学习解决方案。

Abstract: FL has emerged as a transformative paradigm for ITS, notably camera-based Road Condition Classification (RCC). However, by enabling collaboration, FL-based RCC exposes the system to adversarial participants launching Targeted Label-Flipping Attacks (TLFAs). Malicious clients (vehicles) can relabel their local training data (e.g., from an actual uneven road to a wrong smooth road), consequently compromising global model predictions and jeopardizing transportation safety. Existing countermeasures against such poisoning attacks fail to maintain resilient model performance near the necessary attack-free levels in various attack scenarios due to: 1) not tailoring poisoned local model detection to TLFAs, 2) not excluding malicious vehicular clients based on historical behavior, and 3) not remedying the already-corrupted global model after exclusion. To close this research gap, we propose FedTrident, which introduces: 1) neuron-wise analysis for local model misbehavior detection (notably including attack goal identification, critical feature extraction, and GMM-based model clustering and filtering); 2) adaptive client rating for client exclusion according to the local model detection results in each FL round; and 3) machine unlearning for corrupted global model remediation once malicious clients are excluded during FL. Extensive evaluation across diverse FL-RCC models, tasks, and configurations demonstrates that FedTrident can effectively thwart TLFAs, achieving performance comparable to that in attack-free scenarios and outperforming eight baseline countermeasures by 9.49% and 4.47% for the two most critical metrics. Moreover, FedTrident is resilient to various malicious client rates, data heterogeneity levels, complicated multi-task, and dynamic attacks.

</details>


### [24] [Performance Testing of ChaCha20-Poly1305 for Internet of Things and Industrial Control System devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19150)
*Kristján Orri Ragnarsson,Jacky Mallett*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在工业控制系统和物联网设备中，为满足低延迟要求的Modbus和IEC61850等协议添加ChaCha20-Poly1305加密，其加密开销仅占协议延迟要求的极小部分（GOOSE小于7.1%，IEC-60834-1小于3%），证明在低成本边缘设备上实现加密通信是可行的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制系统和物联网设备通常使用未加密或未认证的协议通信，这是历史遗留问题。随着小型设备处理能力的指数级增长和高效加密算法的出现，为ICS协议（如Modbus和IEC61850）添加端设备加密变得更为实际，但这些协议对延迟和延迟方差有严格要求，加密的实际性能影响需要验证。

Method: 在低成本边缘设备（Raspberry PI 4和Intel N95 Mini PC）上测量添加ChaCha20-Poly1305加密到通信周期中的时间开销。研究考虑了现代微处理器动态频率缩放等特性对性能测量的影响。

Result: 在最坏情况下，加密周期仅占GOOSE协议延迟要求的7.1%以下，占IEC-60834-1协议延迟要求的3%以下。这一开销完全在协议规定的延迟要求范围内，证明加密不会影响协议性能。

Conclusion: 在低成本边缘设备上为工业控制系统协议添加ChaCha20-Poly1305加密是可行的，加密开销远低于协议延迟要求，不会影响系统性能，为ICS和物联网设备的安全通信提供了实际解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial Control Systems (ICS), and many simple Internet of Things (IoT) devices, commonly communicate using unencrypted or unauthenticated protocols. For ICS this is an historical carryover since the introduction of these systems predated practical lightweight cryptography. As the processing power of small devices has grown exponentially at the same time as new, more efficient encryption algorithms have become available, end device encryption of communication protocols is becoming much more practical, but is still not widely used with ICS protocols such as Modbus and IEC61850 (GOOSE) which have tight requirements for both latency and variance. Newer micro-processors can also present challenges both to measurement and use, since features such as dynamic frequency scaling can significantly impact performance measurements. In this paper, we measured the time cost of adding encryption into the communication cycle of low-cost edge devices using ChaCha20-Poly1305, and show that in the worst case the encryption cycle took less than 7.1 percent of the latency requirements of Goose, and less than 3% for IEC-60834-1 on Raspberry PI 4, and an Intel N95 Mini PC, which is well within the specified latency requirements for these protocols.

</details>

### [25] [Quotient Geometry and Persistence-Stable Metrics for Swarm Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18041)
*Mark M. Bailey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于多智能体系统配置比较和监测的持久稳定、对称不变的几何表示方法，通过定义商形成空间和形成匹配度量，为群体和星座重构提供理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体和星座重构可以看作是无序点配置在环境空间中的运动。需要开发能够比较和监测多智能体配置数据的几何表示方法，这些方法应具有持久稳定性并保持对称不变性，以应对智能体重新标记和环境对称性的挑战。

Method: 引入商形成空间 $\mathcal{S}_n(M,G)=M^n/(G\times S_n)$ 和形成匹配度量 $d_{M,G}$，通过优化环境对称性 $g\in G$ 和重新标记 $\sigma\in S_n$ 的最坏情况分配误差获得。该度量是Gromov-Hausdorff距离的结构化、物理可解释的松弛，并证明了持久稳定性：$d_B(Φ_k([x]),Φ_k([y]))\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$。

Result: 在$M$紧致/完备且$G$紧致的假设下，$(\mathcal{S}_n(M,G),d_{M,G})$是紧致/完备的，且度量诱导商拓扑；若$M$是测地空间，则商空间也是测地空间，并在碰撞和对称层上呈现分层奇异性。在相位圆模型中证明了条件逆定理：在半圆支撑和间隙标记边距条件下，$H_0$签名与$d_{M,G}$局部双Lipschitz等价。

Conclusion: 该工作为多智能体配置比较提供了持久稳定、对称不变的几何框架，将形成匹配度量与拓扑数据分析的持久同调相结合，为卫星星座和群体形成等应用场景提供了理论工具，并建立了与经典配置空间的联系。

Abstract: Swarm and constellation reconfiguration can be viewed as motion of an unordered point configuration in an ambient space. Here, we provide persistence-stable, symmetry-invariant geometric representations for comparing and monitoring multi-agent configuration data. We introduce a quotient formation space $\mathcal{S}_n(M,G)=M^n/(G\times S_n)$ and a formation matching metric $d_{M,G}$ obtained by optimizing a worst-case assignment error over ambient symmetries $g\in G$ and relabelings $σ\in S_n$. This metric is a structured, physically interpretable relaxation of Gromov--Hausdorff distance: the induced inter-agent metric spaces satisfy $d_{\mathrm{GH}}(X_x,X_y)\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$. Composing this bound with stability of Vietoris--Rips persistence yields $d_B(Φ_k([x]),Φ_k([y]))\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$, providing persistence-stable signatures for reconfiguration monitoring. We analyze the metric geometry of $(\mathcal{S}_n(M,G),d_{M,G})$: under compactness/completeness assumptions on $M$ and compact $G$ it is compact/complete and the metric induces the quotient topology; if $M$ is geodesic then the quotient is geodesic and exhibits stratified singularities along collision and symmetry strata, relating it to classical configuration spaces. We study expressivity of the signatures, identifying symmetry-mismatch and persistence-compression mechanisms for non-injectivity. Finally, in a phase-circle model we prove a conditional inverse theorem: under semicircle support and a gap-labeling margin, the $H_0$ signature is locally bi-Lipschitz to $d_{M,G}$ up to an explicit factor, yielding two-sided control. Examples on $\mathbb{S}^2$ and $\mathbb{T}^m$ illustrate satellite-constellation and formation settings.

</details>


### [26] [HEP Statistical Inference for UAV Fault Detection: CLs, LRT, and SBI Applied to Blade Damage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18546)
*Khushiyant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将粒子物理学的三种统计方法应用于多旋翼无人机螺旋桨故障检测：似然比检验用于二元检测，CLs修正频率方法控制误报率，序列神经后验估计用于定量故障表征。基于转子谐波物理的频谱特征，系统输出二元检测结果、受控误报率和故障严重程度及电机位置的校准后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将粒子物理学中成熟的统计方法迁移到无人机故障检测领域，以解决传统方法在检测精度、误报率控制和故障定量表征方面的不足，提供更可靠、信息更丰富的故障诊断系统。

Method: 1) 似然比检验(LRT)用于二元故障检测；2) CLs修正频率方法控制误报率；3) 序列神经后验估计(SNPE)用于故障严重程度和位置的定量表征。系统基于转子谐波物理提取频谱特征，生成三个输出：二元检测结果、受控误报率和校准后验分布。

Result: 在UAV-FD六旋翼数据集(18次真实飞行，5%和10%叶片损伤)上，留一飞行交叉验证的AUC为0.862±0.007(95% CI: 0.849-0.876)，优于CUSUM(0.708±0.010)、自编码器(0.753±0.009)和LSTM自编码器(0.551)。在5%误报率下，系统检测到93%的显著损伤和81%的轻微损伤。在PADRE四旋翼平台上，仅重新拟合生成模型后AUC达到0.986。SNPE提供完整的故障严重程度后验分布(90%可信区间覆盖率92-100%，MAE 0.012)。每飞行序列检测实现100%故障检测，总体准确率94%。

Conclusion: 粒子物理学的统计方法成功迁移到无人机故障检测，显著提升了检测性能、误报率控制和故障定量表征能力。系统不仅提供二元检测结果，还能输出校准的不确定性估计，为无人机健康监测提供了更可靠、信息更丰富的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper transfers three statistical methods from particle physics to multirotor propeller fault detection: the likelihood ratio test (LRT) for binary detection, the CLs modified frequentist method for false alarm rate control, and sequential neural posterior estimation (SNPE) for quantitative fault characterization. Operating on spectral features tied to rotor harmonic physics, the system returns three outputs: binary detection, controlled false alarm rates, and calibrated posteriors over fault severity and motor location. On UAV-FD, a hexarotor dataset of 18 real flights with 5% and 10% blade damage, leave-one-flight-out cross-validation gives AUC 0.862 +/- 0.007 (95% CI: 0.849--0.876), outperforming CUSUM (0.708 +/- 0.010), autoencoder (0.753 +/- 0.009), and LSTM autoencoder (0.551). At 5% false alarm rate the system detects 93% of significant and 81% of subtle blade damage. On PADRE, a quadrotor platform, AUC reaches 0.986 after refitting only the generative models. SNPE gives a full posterior over fault severity (90% credible interval coverage 92--100%, MAE 0.012), so the output includes uncertainty rather than just a point estimate or fault flag. Per-flight sequential detection achieves 100% fault detection with 94% overall accuracy.

</details>


### [27] [Frayed RoPE and Long Inputs: A Geometric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18017)
*Davis Wertheimer,Aozhong Zhang,Derrick Liu,Penghang Yin,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoPE-ID：通过高频旋转部分通道来修复RoPE在长输入时的性能崩溃问题，保持键/查询聚类分离和汇令牌功能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE在输入长度超过训练长度时会导致性能崩溃，现有分析认为这是通道旋转"超出分布"所致，但缺乏对旋转如何导致病理行为的深入理解

Method: 通过经验和理论分析建立RoPE注意力行为的统一几何理解，发现注意力机制会形成分离的键/查询潜在点云聚类，创建汇令牌。RoPE-ID对部分通道应用高频旋转，保持键/查询聚类分离

Result: 在1B和3B参数Transformer上使用LongBench和RULER信息检索基准测试，证明RoPE-ID能有效处理扩展输入

Conclusion: 从几何角度理解RoPE注意力行为，提出RoPE-ID简单修改，使注意力层能够直接泛化到更长输入，解决RoPE的长输入性能崩溃问题

Abstract: Rotary Positional Embedding (RoPE) is a widely adopted technique for encoding position in language models, which, while effective, causes performance breakdown when input length exceeds training length. Prior analyses assert (rightly) that long inputs cause channels to rotate ``out of distribution,'' but it is not clear how extra rotation relates to or causes pathological behavior. Through empirical and theoretical analysis we advance a unified geometric understanding of attention behavior with RoPE. We find that attention induces tight clustering of separated key and query latent point clouds, allowing for creation of sink tokens: placeholders that allow attention heads to avoid token mixing when not required. RoPE applied to longer inputs damages this key/query cluster separation, producing pathological behavior by inhibiting sink token functionality. From this geometric perspective, we propose RoPE-ID (In Distribution), a straightforward modification that allows attention layers to generalize to longer inputs out of the box: apply RoPE with high frequency to a subset of channels. We demonstrate the effectiveness of RoPE-ID for extended inputs using 1B and 3B parameter Transformers on the LongBench and RULER information retrieval benchmarks.

</details>


### [28] [Engineering Verifiable Modularity in Transformers via Per-Layer Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18029)
*J. Clayton Kerce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出通过架构干预（双流处理、逐层监督、门控注意力）来暴露Transformer的隐藏模块性，使模型对特定注意力头的消融产生可预测的行为变化，将可解释性从被动观察转变为主动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型存在"九头蛇效应"——即使消融被识别为关键组件的注意力头，分布式冗余也会补偿损伤，导致行为变化极小。这使得基于相关性的可解释性变得虚幻，无法预测或控制组件的因果作用。

Method: 提出三种架构干预：1) 双流处理分离token和上下文表示；2) 逐层监督为每个深度提供独立梯度信号；3) 门控注意力正则化趋向离散激活模式。使用逐层监督训练模型，与标准目标训练的架构相同模型进行对比。

Result: 使用逐层监督训练的模型消融效果比标准训练模型大5-23倍，对目标行为的控制杠杆提高4倍。消融效果的标准差从0.63%增加到6.32%，揭示了哪些预测依赖于哪些电路。验证了三个组件：捕获计算动态而非词汇结构的工程特征、提供模块性正控制的架构、展示功能重组的因果实验。

Conclusion: 架构干预（特别是逐层监督）能够暴露Transformer的隐藏模块性，使注意力头消融产生可预测的行为变化，将可解释性从被动观察转变为主动控制，为因果解释和模型控制提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Transformers resist surgical control. Ablating an attention head identified as critical for capitalization produces minimal behavioral change because distributed redundancy compensates for damage. This Hydra effect renders interpretability illusory: we may identify components through correlation, but cannot predict or control their causal role. We demonstrate that architectural interventions can expose hidden modularity. Our approach combines dual-stream processing separating token and contextual representations, per-layer supervision providing independent gradient signal at each depth, and gated attention regularizing toward discrete activation patterns. When trained with per-layer supervision, models produce ablation effects 5 to 23 times larger than architecturally identical controls trained with standard objectives. This enables 4 times greater control leverage on targeted behaviors: scaling identified attention heads produces smooth, predictable changes in model output. The key finding is architectural. Without per-layer supervision, ablation damage concentrates near zero with low variance (Winograd standard deviation 0.63%). With per-layer supervision, effects spread widely (standard deviation 6.32%), revealing which predictions depend on which circuits. The larger variance is not measurement noise but the signature of unmasked modularity. We validate our approach through three components: engineered features that capture computational dynamics rather than vocabulary structure (validated by near-zero correlation with raw activation clustering), an architecture providing positive control for modularity, and causal experiments demonstrating functional reorganization where different tasks route through different attention heads. This es tablishes a methodology for transforming interpretability from passive observation to active control.

</details>


### [29] [Towards Differentiating Between Failures and Domain Shifts in Industrial Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18032)
*Natalia Wojak-Strzelecka,Szymon Bobek,Grzegorz J. Nalepa,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种能够区分数据分布变化是正常领域偏移还是故障的方法，结合变更点检测、监督域适应和可解释AI组件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，数据变化并不总是代表系统异常状态，可能只是正常领域偏移（如处理新产品）。传统方法可能将正常变化误判为故障，因此需要区分故障与健康的数据分布变化以确保系统鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 使用改进的Page-Hinkley变更点检测器识别领域偏移和潜在故障；2) 基于监督域适应的算法进行快速在线异常检测；3) 结合可解释AI组件帮助操作人员最终区分领域偏移和故障。

Result: 方法在钢铁工厂的数据流实验中进行了验证，展示了其区分正常领域偏移和实际故障的能力。

Conclusion: 提出的方法不仅能检测数据分布变化和异常，还能有效区分故障与正常领域偏移，提高了异常检测系统的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Anomaly and failure detection methods are crucial in identifying deviations from normal system operational conditions, which allows for actions to be taken in advance, usually preventing more serious damages. Long-lasting deviations indicate failures, while sudden, isolated changes in the data indicate anomalies. However, in many practical applications, changes in the data do not always represent abnormal system states. Such changes may be recognized incorrectly as failures, while being a normal evolution of the system, e.g. referring to characteristics of starting the processing of a new product, i.e. realizing a domain shift. Therefore, distinguishing between failures and such ''healthy'' changes in data distribution is critical to ensure the practical robustness of the system. In this paper, we propose a method that not only detects changes in the data distribution and anomalies but also allows us to distinguish between failures and normal domain shifts inherent to a given process. The proposed method consists of a modified Page-Hinkley changepoint detector for identification of the domain shift and possible failures and supervised domain-adaptation-based algorithms for fast, online anomaly detection. These two are coupled with an explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) component that aims at helping the human operator to finally differentiate between domain shifts and failures. The method is illustrated by an experiment on a data stream from the steel factory.

</details>


### [30] [MST-Direct: Matching via Sinkhorn Transport for Multivariate Geostatistical Simulation with Complex Non-Linear Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18036)
*Tchalies Bachmann Schmitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MST-Direct算法，基于最优传输理论，使用Sinkhorn算法直接匹配多元分布，同时保持空间相关结构，克服传统方法对线性相关假设的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元地质统计模拟需要忠实再现地质变量间复杂的非线性依赖关系，包括双峰分布、阶跃函数和异方差关系。传统方法如高斯Copula和LU分解假设线性相关结构，通常无法保持这些复杂的联合分布模式。

Method: 提出MST-Direct算法，基于最优传输理论，使用Sinkhorn算法直接匹配多元分布，同时保持空间相关结构。该方法将所有变量作为单个多维向量同时处理，能够在完整联合空间中进行关系匹配，而非依赖成对线性依赖关系。

Result: 该方法能够处理复杂的非线性依赖关系，包括双峰分布、阶跃函数和异方差关系，相比传统方法能更好地保持多元联合分布模式。

Conclusion: MST-Direct为多元地质统计模拟提供了一种新方法，通过最优传输理论直接匹配多元分布，克服了传统线性相关假设的局限性，能够更好地处理复杂的地质变量关系。

Abstract: Multivariate geostatistical simulation requires the faithful reproduction of complex non-linear dependencies among geological variables, including bimodal distributions, step functions, and heteroscedastic relationships. Traditional methods such as the Gaussian Copula and LU Decomposition assume linear correlation structures and often fail to preserve these complex joint distribution patterns. We propose MST-Direct (Matching via Sinkhorn Transport), a novel algorithm based on Optimal Transport theory that uses the Sinkhorn algorithm to directly match multivariate distributions while preserving spatial correlation structures. The method processes all variables simultaneously as a single multidimensional vector, enabling relational matching across the full joint space rather than relying on pairwise linear dependencies.

</details>


### [31] [NANOZK: Layerwise Zero-Knowledge Proofs for Verifiable Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18046)
*Zhaohui Geoffrey Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: METHOD是一个零知识证明系统，用于验证LLM推理过程，确保用户获得的结果确实来自特定模型的计算，防止服务商替换模型或使用缓存响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户查询专有LLM API时，无法获得加密保证来确认实际使用的是声称的模型。服务提供商可能替换为更便宜的模型、应用激进的量化或返回缓存响应，而用户却为前沿能力支付高价却无法检测这些行为。

Method: 利用transformer推理自然分解为独立层计算的特点，开发层间证明框架，每层生成恒定大小的证明。采用查找表近似处理非算术运算（softmax、GELU、LayerNorm），引入Fisher信息引导的验证机制用于不切实际证明所有层的情况。

Result: 在d=128的transformer模型上，METHOD生成5.5KB的恒定大小层证明（2.1KB注意力+3.5KB MLP），验证时间24毫秒。相比EZKL，在d=128时证明大小缩小70倍，证明时间快5.7倍，同时保持形式上的可靠性保证（epsilon < 1e-37）。查找表近似完全保持模型困惑度。

Conclusion: METHOD通过层间分解和查找表近似实现了可验证的LLM推理，解决了服务提供商可能替换模型的问题，在保持模型准确性的同时提供了高效的加密验证机制。

Abstract: When users query proprietary LLM APIs, they receive outputs with no cryptographic assurance that the claimed model was actually used. Service providers could substitute cheaper models, apply aggressive quantization, or return cached responses - all undetectable by users paying premium prices for frontier capabilities. We present METHOD, a zero-knowledge proof system that makes LLM inference verifiable: users can cryptographically confirm that outputs correspond to the computation of a specific model.
  Our approach exploits the fact that transformer inference naturally decomposes into independent layer computations, enabling a layerwise proof framework where each layer generates a constant-size proof regardless of model width. This decomposition sidesteps the scalability barrier facing monolithic approaches and enables parallel proving. We develop lookup table approximations for non-arithmetic operations (softmax, GELU, LayerNorm) that introduce zero measurable accuracy loss, and introduce Fisher information-guided verification for scenarios where proving all layers is impractical.
  On transformer models up to d=128, METHOD generates constant-size layer proofs of 5.5KB (2.1KB attention + 3.5KB MLP) with 24 ms verification time. Compared to EZKL, METHOD achieves 70x smaller proofs and 5.7x faster proving time at d=128, while maintaining formal soundness guarantees (epsilon < 1e-37). Lookup approximations preserve model perplexity exactly, enabling verification without quality compromise.

</details>


### [32] [Signals of Success and Struggle: Early Prediction and Physiological Signatures of Human Performance across Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18798)
*Yufei Cao,Penny Sweetser,Ziyu Chen,Xuanying Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用早期眼动和心电信号预测用户在复杂游戏环境中的后期表现，融合模型达到0.86的平衡准确率，揭示了高低表现者在视觉行为和生理激活上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前瞻性预测用户表现对于及时识别任务执行困难用户至关重要。虽然眼动和心电信号广泛用于表征与表现相关的视觉行为和生理激活，但它们在早期预测和揭示表现差异的生理机制方面的潜力尚未充分探索。

Method: 在具有自然展开复杂性的游戏环境中进行被试内实验，使用早期眼动和心电信号预测后期表现，并检查生理和自我报告的组间差异。采用眼动-心电融合模型和仅眼动模型进行预测。

Result: 眼动-心电融合模型达到0.86的平衡准确率，仅眼动模型显示出相当的预测能力。高表现者表现出更有针对性的注视、调整的视觉采样，随着需求增加维持更稳定的心电激活，并具有更积极的情感体验。

Conclusion: 研究证明了基于早期生理信号的跨会话预测的可行性，为表现差异提供了可解释的见解，并促进了未来的主动干预。高表现者的视觉行为和生理调节策略为设计适应性系统提供了重要参考。

Abstract: User performance is crucial in interactive systems, capturing how effectively users engage with task execution. Prospectively predicting performance enables the timely identification of users struggling with task demands. While ocular and cardiac signals are widely used to characterise performance-relevant visual behaviour and physiological activation, their potential for early prediction and for revealing the physiological mechanisms underlying performance differences remains underexplored. We conducted a within-subject experiment in a game environment with naturally unfolding complexity, using early ocular and cardiac signals to predict later performance and to examine physiological and self-reported group differences. Results show that the ocular-cardiac fusion model achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.86, and the ocular-only model shows comparable predictive power. High performers exhibited targeted gaze and adjusted visual sampling, and sustained more stable cardiac activation as demands intensified, with a more positive affective experience. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of cross-session prediction from early physiology, providing interpretable insights into performance variation and facilitating future proactive intervention.

</details>


### [33] [SLEA-RL: Step-Level Experience Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Agentic Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18079)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLEA-RL：一种在每一步决策时基于当前观察检索相关经验的强化学习框架，解决多轮任务中静态经验检索与动态环境不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于经验增强的方法在多轮工具使用任务中存在局限性：它们只在任务开始时基于初始描述检索经验，并在整个过程中保持不变。然而在多轮设置中，观察值每一步都在变化，这种静态检索会随着任务进展变得越来越不匹配。

Method: SLEA-RL包含三个核心组件：1）步骤级观察聚类，将结构等价的环境状态分组以实现高效的基于聚类的检索；2）自演化经验库，通过基于分数的准入和速率限制提取来提炼成功策略和失败模式；3）带有步骤级信用分配的策略优化，用于在多轮任务中进行细粒度优势估计。经验库通过语义分析而非梯度更新与策略共同演化。

Result: 在长视野多轮智能体基准测试中，SLEA-RL相比各种强化学习基线实现了更优越的性能。

Conclusion: SLEA-RL通过步骤级动态经验检索和自演化经验库，有效解决了多轮任务中静态经验检索的局限性，为LLM智能体在多轮工具使用任务中的经验利用提供了更有效的框架。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown strong results on multi-turn tool-use tasks, yet they operate in isolation during training, failing to leverage experiences accumulated across episodes. Existing experience-augmented methods address this by organizing trajectories into retrievable libraries, but they retrieve experiences only once based on the initial task description and hold them constant throughout the episode. In multi-turn settings where observations change at every step, this static retrieval becomes increasingly mismatched as episodes progress. We propose SLEA-RL (Step-Level Experience-Augmented Reinforcement Learning), a framework that retrieves relevant experiences at each decision step conditioned on the current observation. SLEA-RL operates through three components: (i) step-level observation clustering that groups structurally equivalent environmental states for efficient cluster-indexed retrieval; (ii) a self-evolving experience library that distills successful strategies and failure patterns through score-based admission and rate-limited extraction; and (iii) policy optimization with step-level credit assignment for fine-grained advantage estimation across multi-turn episodes. The experience library evolves alongside the policy through semantic analysis rather than gradient updates. Experiments on long-horizon multi-turn agent benchmarks demonstrate that SLEA-RL achieves superior performance compared to various reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [34] [Enhancing Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning with an Online Refiner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18088)
*Hao Ma,Zhiqiang Pu,Yang Liu,Xiaolin Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态约束方法解决强化学习微调中约束与优化目标的冲突，通过参考模型作为在线精炼器生成最小修正，实现基于输出质量自动调整的约束强度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束在稳定强化学习微调的同时限制了模型发现更好解的能力，存在约束强度与优化目标的固有冲突，需要一种能根据模型能力动态调整的约束机制

Method: 使用参考模型作为在线精炼器，接收微调模型的响应并生成最小修正版本（保留正确内容，仅修正错误），然后通过监督微调损失训练微调模型生成精炼输出，实现动态约束

Result: 在对话和代码生成任务上的实验表明，动态约束方法在保持训练稳定性的同时，显著优于KL正则化和无约束基线，获得更高的任务奖励

Conclusion: 动态约束通过根据模型输出质量自动调整约束强度，有效解决了强化学习微调中约束与优化目标的冲突，实现了更好的性能与稳定性的平衡

Abstract: Constraints are essential for stabilizing reinforcement learning fine-tuning (RFT) and preventing degenerate outputs, yet they inherently conflict with the optimization objective because stronger constraints limit the ability of a fine-tuned model to discover better solutions. We propose \textit{dynamic constraints} that resolve this tension by adapting to the evolving capabilities of the fine-tuned model based on the insight that constraints should only intervene when degenerate outputs occur. We implement this by using a reference model as an \textit{online refiner} that takes the response from the fine-tuned model and generates a minimally corrected version which preserves correct content verbatim while fixing errors. A supervised fine-tuning loss then trains the fine-tuned model to produce the refined output. This mechanism yields a constraint that automatically strengthens or relaxes based on output quality. Experiments on dialogue and code generation show that dynamic constraints outperform both KL regularization and unconstrained baselines, achieving substantially higher task rewards while maintaining training stability.

</details>


### [35] [ARTEMIS: A Neuro Symbolic Framework for Economically Constrained Market Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18107)
*Rahul D Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARTEMIS是一个神经符号框架，通过结合连续时间拉普拉斯神经算子编码器、物理信息正则化的神经随机微分方程和可微符号瓶颈，在量化金融中实现无套利、可解释的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化金融中的深度学习模型通常作为黑箱运行，缺乏可解释性，且未能纳入无套利约束等基本经济原理。需要一种既能保持深度学习强大能力，又能提供透明度和经济合理性的方法。

Method: ARTEMIS框架包含三个核心组件：1) 连续时间拉普拉斯神经算子编码器；2) 通过物理信息损失正则化的神经随机微分方程；3) 可微符号瓶颈，用于提取可解释的交易规则。通过两个新颖的正则化项强制经济合理性：惩罚局部无套利违反的Feynman-Kac PDE残差，以及限制瞬时夏普比率的市场风险价格惩罚。

Result: 在四个数据集（Jane Street、Optiver、Time-IMM和DSLOB）上评估，ARTEMIS在方向性准确率方面达到最先进水平，在DSLOB上为64.96%，在Time-IMM上为96.0%。消融研究表明每个组件都有贡献：移除PDE损失会使方向准确率从64.89%降至50.32%。在Optiver上表现不佳归因于其长序列长度和波动率聚焦的目标。

Conclusion: ARTEMIS通过提供可解释、经济基础扎实的预测，弥合了深度学习能力与量化金融所需透明度之间的差距，为金融建模提供了既强大又透明的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models in quantitative finance often operate as black boxes, lacking interpretability and failing to incorporate fundamental economic principles such as no-arbitrage constraints. This paper introduces ARTEMIS (Arbitrage-free Representation Through Economic Models and Interpretable Symbolics), a novel neuro-symbolic framework combining a continuous-time Laplace Neural Operator encoder, a neural stochastic differential equation regularised by physics-informed losses, and a differentiable symbolic bottleneck that distils interpretable trading rules. The model enforces economic plausibility via two novel regularisation terms: a Feynman-Kac PDE residual penalising local no-arbitrage violations, and a market price of risk penalty bounding the instantaneous Sharpe ratio. We evaluate ARTEMIS against six strong baselines on four datasets: Jane Street, Optiver, Time-IMM, and DSLOB (a synthetic crash regime). Results demonstrate ARTEMIS achieves state-of-the-art directional accuracy, outperforming all baselines on DSLOB (64.96%) and Time-IMM (96.0%). A comprehensive ablation study confirms each component's contribution: removing the PDE loss reduces directional accuracy from 64.89% to 50.32%. Underperformance on Optiver is attributed to its long sequence length and volatility-focused target. By providing interpretable, economically grounded predictions, ARTEMIS bridges the gap between deep learning's power and the transparency demanded in quantitative finance.

</details>


### [36] [Tula: Optimizing Time, Cost, and Generalization in Distributed Large-Batch Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18112)
*Sahil Tyagi,Feiyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tula是一个在线服务，通过结合并行系统建模和统计性能预测，自动优化卷积模型大批次训练的时间、成本和收敛质量，识别最优批次大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式训练通过扩展节点或增加批次大小来提高处理效率，但简单增加批次大小会导致收益递减：训练时间和成本先降后平，形成帕累托曲线拐点。大批次还会因泛化差距导致模型质量下降。需要自动优化时间、成本和收敛质量的方法。

Method: Tula结合并行系统建模与统计性能预测，自动识别最优批次大小。它在线运行，预测训练时间和成本，同时考虑模型、数据和计算资源的特性。

Result: Tula在多个模型上预测训练时间和成本的误差在7.5-14%之间，相比标准大批次训练，实现了最高20倍的整体加速，并在各种视觉任务上平均提高9%的测试准确率。

Conclusion: Tula成功缓解了泛化差距，同时加速了训练，为卷积模型的大批次训练提供了自动化的时间、成本和收敛质量优化方案。

Abstract: Distributed training increases the number of batches processed per iteration either by scaling-out (adding more nodes) or scaling-up (increasing the batch-size). However, the largest configuration does not necessarily yield the best performance. Horizontal scaling introduces additional communication overhead, while vertical scaling is constrained by computation cost and device memory limits. Thus, simply increasing the batch-size leads to diminishing returns: training time and cost decrease initially but eventually plateaus, creating a knee-point in the time/cost versus batch-size pareto curve. The optimal batch-size therefore depends on the underlying model, data and available compute resources. Large batches also suffer from worse model quality due to the well-known generalization gap. In this paper, we present Tula, an online service that automatically optimizes time, cost, and convergence quality for large-batch training of convolutional models. It combines parallel-systems modeling with statistical performance prediction to identify the optimal batch-size. Tula predicts training time and cost within 7.5-14% error across multiple models, and achieves up to 20x overall speedup and improves test accuracy by 9% on average over standard large-batch training on various vision tasks, thus successfully mitigating the generalization gap and accelerating training at the same time.

</details>


### [37] [VC-Soup: Value-Consistency Guided Multi-Value Alignment for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18113)
*Hefei Xu,Le Wu,Yu Wang,Min Hou,Han Wu,Zhen Zhang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VC-soup：基于价值一致性的数据过滤与参数融合框架，通过价值一致性度量筛选偏好数据，训练平滑策略模型，再线性组合并帕累托过滤，有效缓解多价值对齐中的冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在内容生成、交互和决策中的广泛应用，将其与人类价值观对齐已成为可信AI的核心目标。多价值对齐面临两大挑战：1) 为每个价值组合单独训练模型成本过高；2) 价值冲突严重降低对齐性能。现有方法如奖励重加权、基于提示的监督微调和模型融合仍难以实现不同人类价值观之间的有利权衡。

Method: 提出VC-soup框架：1) 设计基于余弦相似度的价值一致性度量，量化每个偏好对的跨价值一致性；2) 过滤掉各价值数据集中低一致性的偏好对；3) 在剩余数据上训练获得平滑、价值一致的策略模型，更好地保持线性模式连接性；4) 线性组合这些策略模型，并应用跨价值的帕累托过滤，获得平衡的多价值性能解。

Result: 大量实验和理论分析表明，VC-soup能有效缓解冲突，在多价值对齐任务上持续优于现有方法，实现了更好的跨价值权衡。

Conclusion: 从数据价值一致性角度重新审视多价值对齐问题，提出的VC-soup框架通过数据过滤和参数融合有效解决了多价值对齐中的冲突和成本问题，为可信AI中的多价值对齐提供了新思路。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly shape content generation, interaction, and decision-making across the Web, aligning them with human values has become a central objective in trustworthy AI. This challenge becomes even more pronounced when aligning multiple, potentially conflicting human values. Although recent approaches, such as reward reweighting, prompt-based supervised fine-tuning, and model merging, attempt to tackle multi-value alignment, they still face two major limitations: (1) training separate models for each value combination is prohibitively expensive; (2) value conflicts substantially degrade alignment performance. These limitations make it difficult to achieve favorable trade-offs across diverse human values. To address these challenges, we revisit multi-value alignment from the perspective of value consistency in data and propose VC-soup, a data filtering and parameter merging framework grounded in value-consistent learning. We first design a value consistency metric based on the cosine similarity between the reward-gap vector of each preference pair and an all-ones vector, which quantifies its cross-value coherence. We then filter out low-consistency preference pairs in each value dataset and train on the remaining data to obtain smooth, value-consistent policy models that better preserve linear mode connectivity. Finally, we linearly combine these policies and apply Pareto filtering across values to obtain solutions with balanced multi-value performance. Extensive experiments and theoretical analysis demonstrate that VC-soup effectively mitigates conflicts and consistently outperforms existing multi-value alignment methods.

</details>
