Method: 为Adam优化器建立先验一致有界性（uniform a priori bounds），证明在强凸随机优化问题中，Adam的参数不会发散到无穷大。基于这一有界性，首次提供了无条件的误差分析。

Result: 成功证明了Adam在强凸随机优化问题中的参数先验有界性，从而首次为一大类强凸随机优化问题提供了无条件的误差分析结果，克服了先前分析中需要假设参数有界的限制。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了Adam优化器理论分析的重要空白，为强凸随机优化问题提供了首个无条件收敛分析，为Adam的理论理解奠定了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: The adaptive moment estimation (Adam) optimizer proposed by Kingma & Ba (2014) is presumably the most popular stochastic gradient descent (SGD) optimization method for the training of deep neural networks (DNNs) in artificial intelligence (AI) systems. Despite its groundbreaking success in the training of AI systems, it still remains an open research problem to provide a complete error analysis of Adam, not only for optimizing DNNs but even when applied to strongly convex stochastic optimization problems (SOPs). Previous error analysis results for strongly convex SOPs in the literature provide conditional convergence analyses that rely on the assumption that Adam does not diverge to infinity but remains uniformly bounded. It is the key contribution of this work to establish uniform a priori bounds for Adam and, thereby, to provide -- for the first time -- an unconditional error analysis for Adam for a large class of strongly convex SOPs.

</details>


### [79] [Context Bootstrapped Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18953)
*Saaket Agashe,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,Ramana Kompella,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CBRL通过随机预置few-shot演示来增强RLVR训练，采用从高到零的课程学习概率，提升探索效率并内化推理模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR存在探索效率低的问题，特别是在需要获取新颖推理模式或领域特定知识的任务中，模型难以生成成功的rollout，导致学习信号微弱

Method: 提出上下文引导强化学习(CBRL)，在训练提示中随机预置few-shot演示，注入概率采用从高开始引导早期探索，然后逐渐衰减到零的课程学习策略，迫使策略内化演示中的推理模式而非在测试时依赖它们

Result: 在两个模型家族和五个Reasoning Gym任务上验证了CBRL，结果显示CBRL持续提升成功率，提供更好的探索效率，且算法无关。在Q领域特定编程语言上的应用也证明了其实用性

Conclusion: CBRL通过引导性演示和课程学习有效解决了RLVR的探索效率问题，使模型能够内化推理模式，在无需外部辅助的情况下成功完成任务

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) suffers from exploration inefficiency, where models struggle to generate successful rollouts, resulting in minimal learning signal. This challenge is particularly severe for tasks that require the acquisition of novel reasoning patterns or domain-specific knowledge. To address this, we propose Context Bootstrapped Reinforcement Learning (CBRL), which augments RLVR training by stochastically prepending few-shot demonstrations to training prompts. The injection probability follows a curriculum that starts high to bootstrap early exploration, then anneals to zero so the model must ultimately succeed without assistance. This forces the policy to internalize reasoning patterns from the demonstrations rather than relying on them at test time. We validate CBRL across two model families and five Reasoning Gym tasks. Our results demonstrate that CBRL consistently improves success rate, provides better exploration efficiency, and is algorithm-agnostic. We further demonstrate CBRL's practical applicability on Q, a domain-specific programming language that diverges significantly from mainstream language conventions.

</details>


### [80] [BVSIMC: Bayesian Variable Selection-Guided Inductive Matrix Completion for Improved and Interpretable Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18957)
*Sijian Fan,Liyan Xiong,Dayuan Wang,Guoshuai Cai,Ray Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BVSIMC方法，通过贝叶斯变量选择从药物发现的侧信息中筛选特征，学习稀疏潜在嵌入，提高预测准确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中，侧信息（如药物化学性质和疾病基因组信息）能显著提升预测性能，但这些特征通常相关性差异大、噪声多且高维，需要有效的方法进行特征筛选。

Method: 提出贝叶斯变量选择引导的归纳矩阵补全（BVSIMC），这是一种新的贝叶斯模型，能够从侧特征中进行变量选择，通过学习稀疏潜在嵌入来改进预测。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上，BVSIMC在预测性能上优于多种最先进方法。在两个真实药物发现应用中，BVSIMC进一步揭示了最具临床意义的侧特征。

Conclusion: BVSIMC通过贝叶斯变量选择有效处理药物发现中的高维噪声侧信息，不仅提升预测准确性，还增强了模型的可解释性，为药物发现提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Recent advances in drug discovery have demonstrated that incorporating side information (e.g., chemical properties about drugs and genomic information about diseases) often greatly improves prediction performance. However, these side features can vary widely in relevance and are often noisy and high-dimensional. We propose Bayesian Variable Selection-Guided Inductive Matrix Completion (BVSIMC), a new Bayesian model that enables variable selection from side features in drug discovery. By learning sparse latent embeddings, BVSIMC improves both predictive accuracy and interpretability. We validate our method through simulation studies and two drug discovery applications: 1) prediction of drug resistance in Mycobacterium tuberculosis, and 2) prediction of new drug-disease associations in computational drug repositioning. On both synthetic and real data, BVSIMC outperforms several other state-of-the-art methods in terms of prediction. In our two real examples, BVSIMC further reveals the most clinically meaningful side features.

</details>


### [81] [Maximum-Entropy Exploration with Future State-Action Visitation Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18965)
*Adrien Bolland,Gaspard Lambrechts,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的探索奖励方法，通过最大化轨迹内特征多样性来改进探索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大熵强化学习通常通过提供与熵函数成比例的内在奖励来激励智能体探索。本文研究基于未来时间步访问的状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的内在奖励，旨在改进探索策略。

Method: 提出内在奖励与未来时间步访问的状态-动作特征折扣分布熵成比例。证明该内在奖励的期望和是初始状态开始轨迹中访问的状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的下界，且该分布是收缩算子的不动点，可进行离策略估计。

Result: 新目标在单个轨迹内改进了特征的访问多样性，但以略微减少不同轨迹间特征的期望访问为代价。在纯探索智能体学习中提高了收敛速度，在大多数基准测试中控制性能保持相似。

Conclusion: 基于状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的内在奖励方法能有效改进探索策略，特别是在单个轨迹内的特征多样性方面，同时保持了控制性能，为最大熵强化学习提供了新的探索机制。

Abstract: Maximum entropy reinforcement learning motivates agents to explore states and actions to maximize the entropy of some distribution, typically by providing additional intrinsic rewards proportional to that entropy function. In this paper, we study intrinsic rewards proportional to the entropy of the discounted distribution of state-action features visited during future time steps. This approach is motivated by two results. First, we show that the expected sum of these intrinsic rewards is a lower bound on the entropy of the discounted distribution of state-action features visited in trajectories starting from the initial states, which we relate to an alternative maximum entropy objective. Second, we show that the distribution used in the intrinsic reward definition is the fixed point of a contraction operator and can therefore be estimated off-policy. Experiments highlight that the new objective leads to improved visitation of features within individual trajectories, in exchange for slightly reduced visitation of features in expectation over different trajectories, as suggested by the lower bound. It also leads to improved convergence speed for learning exploration-only agents. Control performance remains similar across most methods on the considered benchmarks.

</details>


### [82] [Best-of-Both-Worlds Multi-Dueling Bandits: Unified Algorithms for Stochastic and Adversarial Preferences under Condorcet and Borda Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18972)
*S. Akash,Pratik Gajane,Jawar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个适用于随机和对抗环境的"两全其美"多决斗赌博机算法，针对Condorcet和Borda两种目标设置，无需预先知道所处环境类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多决斗赌博机在排名和推荐系统中自然出现，但一个基本问题尚未解决：是否存在单一算法能在随机和对抗环境中都表现最优，而无需知道面临哪种环境？

Method: 针对Condorcet设置，提出MetaDueling黑盒归约，将多路胜者反馈转换为无偏成对信号；针对Borda设置，提出AlgBorda算法。两者都无需预先知道环境类型。

Result: MetaDueling结合Versatile-DB实现O(√KT)对抗伪遗憾和实例最优O(∑(logT/Δ_i))随机伪遗憾；AlgBorda实现O(K²logKT + Klog²T + ∑(KlogKT/(Δ_i^B)²))随机遗憾和O(K√(TlogKT) + K¹/³T²/³(logK)¹/³)对抗遗憾。

Conclusion: 首次为多决斗赌博机提供了同时适用于随机和对抗环境的"两全其美"算法，填补了该领域的重要空白，并通过匹配下界证明了最优性。

Abstract: Multi-dueling bandits, where a learner selects $m \geq 2$ arms per round and observes only the winner, arise naturally in many applications including ranking and recommendation systems, yet a fundamental question has remained open: can a single algorithm perform optimally in both stochastic and adversarial environments, without knowing which regime it faces? We answer this affirmatively, providing the first best-of-both-worlds algorithms for multi-dueling bandits under both Condorcet and Borda objectives. For the Condorcet setting, we propose \texttt{MetaDueling}, a black-box reduction that converts any dueling bandit algorithm into a multi-dueling bandit algorithm by transforming multi-way winner feedback into an unbiased pairwise signal. Instantiating our reduction with \texttt{Versatile-DB} yields the first best-of-both-worlds algorithm for multi-dueling bandits: it achieves $O(\sqrt{KT})$ pseudo-regret against adversarial preferences and the instance-optimal $O\!\left(\sum_{i \neq a^\star} \frac{\log T}{Δ_i}\right)$ pseudo-regret under stochastic preferences, both simultaneously and without prior knowledge of the regime. For the Borda setting, we propose \AlgBorda, a stochastic-and-adversarial algorithm that achieves $O\left(K^2 \log KT + K \log^2 T + \sum_{i: Δ_i^{\mathrm{B}} > 0} \frac{K\log KT}{(Δ_i^{\mathrm{B}})^2}\right)$ regret in stochastic environments and $O\left(K \sqrt{T \log KT} + K^{1/3} T^{2/3} (\log K)^{1/3}\right)$ regret against adversaries, again without prior knowledge of the regime. We complement our upper bounds with matching lower bounds for the Condorcet setting. For the Borda setting, our upper bounds are near-optimal with respect to the lower bounds (within a factor of $K$) and match the best-known results in the literature.

</details>


### [83] [Foundations of Schrödinger Bridges for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18992)
*Sophia Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指南系统阐述了薛定谔桥作为生成建模统一框架的数学基础，连接了扩散模型、基于分数的模型和流匹配等方法，提供了从第一原理构建薛定谔桥的完整工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成建模框架（包括扩散模型、基于分数的模型和流匹配）的核心任务是通过概率空间中的随机路径将简单先验分布转换为复杂目标分布。需要统一的数学原理来理解这些方法的深层联系，薛定谔桥提供了这样的理论基础。

Method: 基于最优传输、随机控制和路径空间优化的数学工具，发展了薛定谔桥问题的动态表述。从第一原理构建薛定谔桥的完整工具包，展示如何将这些构造转化为广义和特定任务的计算方法。

Result: 建立了薛定谔桥与生成建模的直接联系，提供了统一的数学框架来理解扩散模型、基于分数的模型和流匹配等方法。开发了从第一原理构建薛定谔桥的系统方法。

Conclusion: 薛定谔桥为现代生成建模提供了统一的理论基础，通过最优传输、随机控制和路径空间优化的数学工具，能够系统地理解和构建各种生成模型，并为开发新的计算方法提供了框架。

Abstract: At the core of modern generative modeling frameworks, including diffusion models, score-based models, and flow matching, is the task of transforming a simple prior distribution into a complex target distribution through stochastic paths in probability space. Schrödinger bridges provide a unifying principle underlying these approaches, framing the problem as determining an optimal stochastic bridge between marginal distribution constraints with minimal-entropy deviations from a pre-defined reference process. This guide develops the mathematical foundations of the Schrödinger bridge problem, drawing on optimal transport, stochastic control, and path-space optimization, and focuses on its dynamic formulation with direct connections to modern generative modeling. We build a comprehensive toolkit for constructing Schrödinger bridges from first principles, and show how these constructions give rise to generalized and task-specific computational methods.

</details>


### [84] [When Differential Privacy Meets Wireless Federated Learning: An Improved Analysis for Privacy and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19040)
*Chen Yaoling,Liang Hao,Tu Xiaotong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对差分隐私无线联邦学习（DPWFL）进行了全面的隐私和收敛性分析，解决了现有工作中隐私损失量化不精确、收敛分析依赖严格凸性假设或忽略梯度裁剪影响等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPWFL框架在精确量化隐私损失方面存在基础性问题，且收敛分析要么依赖限制性的凸性假设，要么忽略梯度裁剪的影响。这些局限性阻碍了对非凸损失函数下DPWFL系统的深入理解。

Method: 提出了一种针对一般光滑非凸损失目标的DPWFL综合分析方法，明确考虑了设备选择和mini-batch采样机制，并建立了包含梯度裁剪的收敛保证。

Result: 分析表明隐私损失可以收敛到一个常数而非随迭代次数发散，推导出了明确的隐私-效用权衡关系，数值结果验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 该研究为DPWFL提供了更精确的隐私损失表征和更现实的收敛分析框架，特别适用于非凸损失场景，为实际部署提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Differentially private wireless federated learning (DPWFL) is a promising framework for protecting sensitive user data. However, foundational questions on how to precisely characterize privacy loss remain open, and existing work is further limited by convergence analyses that rely on restrictive convexity assumptions or ignore the effect of gradient clipping. To overcome these issues, we present a comprehensive analysis of privacy and convergence for DPWFL with general smooth non-convex loss objectives. Our analysis explicitly incorporates both device selection and mini-batch sampling, and shows that the privacy loss can converge to a constant rather than diverge with the number of iterations. Moreover, we establish convergence guarantees with gradient clipping and derive an explicit privacy-utility trade-off. Numerical results validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [85] [Communication-Efficient and Robust Multi-Modal Federated Learning via Latent-Space Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19067)
*Mohamed Badi,Chaouki Ben Issaid,Mehdi Bennis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMFed是一个通信高效的多模态联邦学习框架，使用可学习投影矩阵生成压缩潜在表示，通过潜在空间正则化对齐客户端表示，在人类活动识别基准上实现竞争性精度且开销最小


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习（FL）支持分布式设备协作训练模型而无需共享原始数据，但将FL应用于多模态设置面临显著挑战。客户端通常具有异构的模态和模型架构，难以在保护隐私和最小化通信成本的同时有效对齐特征空间。

Method: 引入CoMFed框架：1）使用可学习投影矩阵生成压缩的潜在表示；2）通过潜在空间正则化对齐不同客户端间的表示；3）提高跨模态一致性并增强对异常值的鲁棒性。

Result: 在人类活动识别基准测试中，CoMFed实现了竞争性的准确率，同时保持了最小的通信开销。

Conclusion: CoMFed框架有效解决了多模态联邦学习中的通信效率和特征对齐问题，在保护隐私的同时实现了高性能的多模态协作学习。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, but applying FL to multi-modal settings introduces significant challenges. Clients typically possess heterogeneous modalities and model architectures, making it difficult to align feature spaces efficiently while preserving privacy and minimizing communication costs. To address this, we introduce CoMFed, a Communication-Efficient Multi-Modal Federated Learning framework that uses learnable projection matrices to generate compressed latent representations. A latent-space regularizer aligns these representations across clients, improving cross-modal consistency and robustness to outliers. Experiments on human activity recognition benchmarks show that CoMFed achieves competitive accuracy with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [86] [On Optimizing Multimodal Jailbreaks for Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19127)
*Aravind Krishnan,Karolina Stańczak,Dietrich Klakow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JAMA联合音频-文本多模态攻击框架，通过同时扰动语音和文本模态实现多模态越狱，相比单模态攻击成功率提升1.5-10倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口语语言模型(SLMs)继承了LLM的安全漏洞并扩展了攻击面，现有攻击多为单模态（仅文本或音频），需要探索多模态联合攻击方法

Method: 提出JAMA联合多模态优化框架：文本部分使用Greedy Coordinate Gradient(GCG)，音频部分使用Projected Gradient Descent(PGD)，同时扰动两种模态；还开发了顺序近似方法加速攻击

Result: 在四个SOTA SLM和四种音频类型上评估，JAMA比单模态越狱成功率提高1.5-10倍；顺序近似方法使攻击速度提升4-6倍

Conclusion: 单模态安全性不足以确保SLM的鲁棒性，多模态联合攻击揭示了SLM的新安全漏洞，需要开发更强的多模态防御机制

Abstract: As Spoken Language Models (SLMs) integrate speech and text modalities, they inherit the safety vulnerabilities of their LLM backbone and an expanded attack surface. SLMs have been previously shown to be susceptible to jailbreaking, where adversarial prompts induce harmful responses. Yet existing attacks largely remain unimodal, optimizing either text or audio in isolation. We explore gradient-based multimodal jailbreaks by introducing JAMA (Joint Audio-text Multimodal Attack), a joint multimodal optimization framework combining Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) for text and Projected Gradient Descent (PGD) for audio, to simultaneously perturb both modalities. Evaluations across four state-of-the-art SLMs and four audio types demonstrate that JAMA surpasses unimodal jailbreak rate by 1.5x to 10x. We analyze the operational dynamics of this joint attack and show that a sequential approximation method makes it 4x to 6x faster. Our findings suggest that unimodal safety is insufficient for robust SLMs. The code and data are available at https://repos.lsv.uni-saarland.de/akrishnan/multimodal-jailbreak-slm

</details>


### [87] [Hierarchical Latent Structure Learning through Online Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19139)
*Ines Aitsahalia,Kiyohito Iigaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HOLMES模型：一种通过在线推理学习层次潜在结构的计算框架，结合嵌套中餐馆过程先验和序列蒙特卡洛推理，实现无监督的层次表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习系统需要在经验泛化和任务相关细节区分之间取得平衡。在线潜在原因模型支持增量推理但假设扁平分区，而层次贝叶斯模型能捕捉多级结构但通常需要离线推理。需要一种能在线学习层次潜在结构的计算框架。

Method: 提出HOLMES模型，结合嵌套中餐馆过程先验的变体和序列蒙特卡洛推理，实现无监督的、试验到试验的层次潜在表示推理。该框架支持在线学习而不需要显式的潜在结构监督。

Result: 在模拟中，HOLMES与扁平模型的预测性能相当，但学习了更紧凑的表示，支持对高层潜在类别的一次性迁移。在具有嵌套时间结构的上下文依赖任务中，HOLMES相对于扁平模型改进了结果预测。

Conclusion: HOLMES为在序列数据中发现层次结构提供了一个可处理的计算框架，平衡了泛化和区分需求，支持在线层次潜在结构学习。

Abstract: Learning systems must balance generalization across experiences with discrimination of task-relevant details. Effective learning therefore requires representations that support both. Online latent-cause models support incremental inference but assume flat partitions, whereas hierarchical Bayesian models capture multilevel structure but typically require offline inference. We introduce the Hierarchical Online Learning of Multiscale Experience Structure (HOLMES) model, a computational framework for hierarchical latent structure learning through online inference. HOLMES combines a variation on the nested Chinese Restaurant Process prior with sequential Monte Carlo inference to perform tractable trial-by-trial inference over hierarchical latent representations without explicit supervision over the latent structure. In simulations, HOLMES matched the predictive performance of flat models while learning more compact representations that supported one-shot transfer to higher-level latent categories. In a context-dependent task with nested temporal structure, HOLMES also improved outcome prediction relative to flat models. These results provide a tractable computational framework for discovering hierarchical structure in sequential data.

</details>


### [88] [SHAPCA: Consistent and Interpretable Explanations for Machine Learning Models on Spectroscopy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19141)
*Mingxing Zhang,Nicola Rossberg,Simone Innocente,Katarzyna Komolibus,Rekha Gautam,Barry O'Sullivan,Luca Longo,Andrea Visentin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SHAPCA框架，结合主成分分析和SHAP解释方法，为高维光谱数据提供可解释的机器学习预测解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光谱数据的高维度和强共线性给模型可解释性带来挑战，影响解释的稳定性和一致性，阻碍在临床和安全关键场景中的可信应用。

Method: 提出SHAPCA框架，结合主成分分析进行降维，使用SHAP进行后验解释，在原始输入空间提供解释，支持全局和局部分析视角。

Result: 数值分析表明该方法提高了结果的可解释性，并在不同训练运行中获得了更好的一致性。

Conclusion: SHAPCA框架能够为光谱数据的机器学习模型提供稳定、一致且可解释的预测解释，有助于实践者理解模型决策并将其与生物组分关联。

Abstract: In recent years, machine learning models have been increasingly applied to spectroscopic datasets for chemical and biomedical analysis. For their successful adoption, particularly in clinical and safety-critical settings, professionals and researchers must be able to understand and trust the reasoning behind model predictions. However, the inherently high dimensionality and strong collinearity of spectroscopy data pose a fundamental challenge to model explainability. These properties not only complicate model training but also undermine the stability and consistency of explanations, leading to fluctuations in feature importance across repeated training runs. Feature extraction techniques have been used to reduce the input dimensionality; these new features hinder the connection between the prediction and the original signal. This study proposes SHAPCA, an explainable machine learning pipeline that combines Principal Component Analysis (for dimensionality reduction) and Shapely Additive exPlanations (for post hoc explanation) to provide explanations in the original input space, which a practitioner can interpret and link back to the biological components. The proposed framework enables analysis from both global and local perspectives, revealing the spectral bands that drive overall model behaviour as well as the instance-specific features that influence individual predictions. Numerical analysis demonstrated the interpretability of the results and greater consistency across different runs.

</details>


### [89] [Rigorous Error Certification for Neural PDE Solvers: From Empirical Residuals to Solution Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19165)
*Amartya Mukherjee,Maxwell Fitzsimmons,David C. Del Rey Fernández,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立了PINNs的泛化误差理论框架，证明了当神经近似位于解空间的紧子集时，残差误差的消失能保证收敛到真实解，并提供了确定性概率收敛结果和经过认证的泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PDE不确定性量化基于离散化理论，通过网格细化控制解误差。PINNs从根本上偏离这一范式：通过在配置点最小化残差损失来近似解，引入了优化、采样、表示和过拟合等新误差源。因此，解空间中的泛化误差仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 建立理论框架，证明当神经近似位于解空间的紧子集时，残差误差的消失能保证收敛到真实解。推导确定性概率收敛结果，提供经过认证的泛化界，将残差、边界和初始误差转化为显式的解误差保证。

Result: 建立了连接残差控制与解空间误差的泛化界。证明了在紧性条件下，消失的残差误差能保证收敛到真实PDE解。提供了确定性概率收敛结果和经过认证的泛化误差界限。

Conclusion: 该研究为PINNs提供了首个严格的泛化误差理论框架，解决了传统离散化理论与PINNs方法之间的理论差距，为PINNs的可靠性和不确定性量化奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification for partial differential equations is traditionally grounded in discretization theory, where solution error is controlled via mesh/grid refinement. Physics-informed neural networks fundamentally depart from this paradigm: they approximate solutions by minimizing residual losses at collocation points, introducing new sources of error arising from optimization, sampling, representation, and overfitting. As a result, the generalization error in the solution space remains an open problem.
  Our main theoretical contribution establishes generalization bounds that connect residual control to solution-space error. We prove that when neural approximations lie in a compact subset of the solution space, vanishing residual error guarantees convergence to the true solution. We derive deterministic and probabilistic convergence results and provide certified generalization bounds translating residual, boundary, and initial errors into explicit solution error guarantees.

</details>


### [90] [DyMoE: Dynamic Expert Orchestration with Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient MoE Inference on Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19172)
*Yuegui Huang,Zhiyuan Fang,Weiqi Luo,Ruoyu Wu,Wuhui Chen,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DyMoE是一个动态混合精度量化框架，用于在资源受限的边缘平台上实现高性能的MoE模型推理，通过重要性感知优先级、深度自适应调度和前瞻预取技术，显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在边缘平台实时推理面临内存占用大和I/O开销高的挑战，现有静态方法在延迟-精度权衡上过于僵化，且观察到专家重要性存在高度偏斜和深度依赖性。

Method: 提出DyMoE框架，包含三个核心技术：1) 重要性感知优先级：运行时动态量化专家；2) 深度自适应调度：保护关键层的语义完整性；3) 前瞻预取：重叠I/O停顿。

Result: 在商用边缘硬件上，DyMoE相比最先进的卸载基线，将首词时间(TTFT)减少3.44x-22.7x，每输出词时间(TPOT)加速最高达14.58x，实现了实时且保持精度的MoE推理。

Conclusion: DyMoE通过动态混合精度量化有效解决了MoE模型在边缘设备上的内存和I/O瓶颈，实现了实时、高精度的推理，为资源受限环境下的MoE部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Despite the computational efficiency of MoE models, the excessive memory footprint and I/O overhead inherent in multi-expert architectures pose formidable challenges for real-time inference on resource-constrained edge platforms. While existing static methods struggle with a rigid latency-accuracy trade-off, we observe that expert importance is highly skewed and depth-dependent. Motivated by these insights, we propose DyMoE, a dynamic mixed-precision quantization framework designed for high-performance edge inference. Leveraging insights into expert importance skewness and depth-dependent sensitivity, DyMoE introduces: (1) importance-aware prioritization to dynamically quantize experts at runtime; (2) depth-adaptive scheduling to preserve semantic integrity in critical layers; and (3) look-ahead prefetching to overlap I/O stalls. Experimental results on commercial edge hardware show that DyMoE reduces Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) by 3.44x-22.7x and up to a 14.58x speedup in Time-Per-Output-Token (TPOT) compared to state-of-the-art offloading baselines, enabling real-time, accuracy-preserving MoE inference on resource-constrained edge devices.

</details>


### [91] [MIDST Challenge at SaTML 2025: Membership Inference over Diffusion-models-based Synthetic Tabular data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19185)
*Masoumeh Shafieinejad,Xi He,Mahshid Alinoori,John Jewell,Sana Ayromlou,Wei Pang,Veronica Chatrath,Garui Sharma,Deval Pandya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIDST挑战赛评估扩散模型生成的合成表格数据在成员推理攻击下的隐私保护效果，开发了针对扩散模型的黑盒和白盒攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据常被视为数据匿名化和隐私保护数据发布的理想解决方案，但扩散模型生成的表格数据的隐私弹性（特别是对成员推理攻击的抵抗力）尚未得到充分探索。

Method: MIDST挑战赛通过开发针对扩散模型的新型黑盒和白盒成员推理攻击方法，评估合成表格数据的隐私增益。针对混合数据类型单表和具有互连约束的多关系表，探索了多种目标模型。

Result: MIDST挑战赛开发了专门针对扩散模型的黑盒和白盒成员推理攻击方法，为全面评估扩散模型生成合成表格数据的隐私效能提供了工具。

Conclusion: 扩散模型生成的合成表格数据的隐私保护效果需要通过专门的成员推理攻击方法进行系统评估，MIDST挑战赛为此提供了重要的评估框架和工具。

Abstract: Synthetic data is often perceived as a silver-bullet solution to data anonymization and privacy-preserving data publishing. Drawn from generative models like diffusion models, synthetic data is expected to preserve the statistical properties of the original dataset while remaining resilient to privacy attacks. Recent developments of diffusion models have been effective on a wide range of data types, but their privacy resilience, particularly for tabular formats, remains largely unexplored. MIDST challenge sought a quantitative evaluation of the privacy gain of synthetic tabular data generated by diffusion models, with a specific focus on its resistance to membership inference attacks (MIAs). Given the heterogeneity and complexity of tabular data, multiple target models were explored for MIAs, including diffusion models for single tables of mixed data types and multi-relational tables with interconnected constraints. MIDST inspired the development of novel black-box and white-box MIAs tailored to these target diffusion models as a key outcome, enabling a comprehensive evaluation of their privacy efficacy. The MIDST GitHub repository is available at https://github.com/VectorInstitute/MIDST

</details>


### [92] [Improving RCT-Based Treatment Effect Estimation Under Covariate Mismatch via Calibrated Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19186)
*Amir Asiaee,Samhita Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALM方法通过嵌入对齐解决RCT与观察性研究间的协变量不匹配问题，无需插补即可估计异质性处理效应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验(RCT)是估计异质性处理效应的金标准，但通常统计功效不足；大型观察性研究(OS)可补充RCT进行CATE估计，但面临协变量不匹配的关键障碍：两种数据源测量不同且仅部分重叠的协变量

Method: 提出CALM方法：学习将每个数据源特征映射到共同表示空间的嵌入，将OS结果模型转移到RCT嵌入空间，并使用试验数据进行校准，保持随机化的因果识别性。包含基于校准的线性变体和神经变体

Result: 有限样本风险界限分解为对齐误差、结果模型复杂度和校准复杂度项，识别嵌入对齐何时优于插补。线性变体提供负迁移保护，神经变体在严重分布偏移下可能脆弱。在稀疏线性模型下，嵌入方法严格泛化了插补。51个设置的模拟证实：(1)基于校准的方法在线性CATE下等效；(2)神经嵌入变体在所有22个非线性机制设置中均以较大优势胜出

Conclusion: CALM通过嵌入对齐有效解决了RCT与观察性研究间的协变量不匹配问题，为异质性处理效应估计提供了优于传统插补方法的新框架，特别在非线性机制下表现优异

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are the gold standard for estimating heterogeneous treatment effects, yet they are often underpowered for detecting effect heterogeneity. Large observational studies (OS) can supplement RCTs for conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimation, but a key barrier is covariate mismatch: the two sources measure different, only partially overlapping, covariates. We propose CALM (Calibrated ALignment under covariate Mismatch), which bypasses imputation by learning embeddings that map each source's features into a common representation space. OS outcome models are transferred to the RCT embedding space and calibrated using trial data, preserving causal identification from randomization. Finite-sample risk bounds decompose into alignment error, outcome-model complexity, and calibration complexity terms, identifying when embedding alignment outperforms imputation. Under the calibration-based linear variant, the framework provides protection against negative transfer; the neural variant can be vulnerable under severe distributional shift. Under sparse linear models, the embedding approach strictly generalizes imputation. Simulations across 51 settings confirm that (i) calibration-based methods are equivalent for linear CATEs, and (ii) the neural embedding variant wins all 22 nonlinear-regime settings with large margins.

</details>


### [93] [Robustness, Cost, and Attack-Surface Concentration in Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19204)
*Julian Allagan,Mohamed Elbakary,Zohreh Safari,Weizheng Gao,Gabrielle Morgan,Essence Morgan,Vladimir Deriglazov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了钓鱼网站检测器在对抗性特征操纵下的鲁棒性，提出了基于成本的规避框架和三个诊断指标，发现模型鲁棒性主要受特征经济性而非模型复杂度影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钓鱼检测器在独立同分布评估下能达到近乎完美的准确率，但在实际部署中面临特征被攻击者操纵的风险。需要研究检测器在预算约束的特征编辑攻击下的鲁棒性差距。

Method: 提出了成本感知的规避框架，建模离散、单调的特征编辑，并引入三个诊断指标：最小规避成本(MEC)、规避生存率S(B)和鲁棒性集中指数(RCI)。在UCI钓鱼网站数据集上评估了逻辑回归、随机森林、梯度提升树和XGBoost等模型。

Result: 所有模型在静态评估下AUC≥0.979，但在预算约束的规避攻击下，鲁棒性在不同架构间趋于一致：完整特征下中位MEC为2，超过80%的最小成本规避集中在三个低成本表面特征。特征限制仅在移除所有主导低成本转换时才能改善鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 钓鱼检测的对抗鲁棒性主要由特征经济性而非模型复杂度决定。如果正类实例中有一部分可以通过最小成本c_min的单特征转换实现规避，那么任何分类器都无法在不修改特征表示或成本模型的情况下提高相应的MEC分位数。

Abstract: Phishing detectors built on engineered website features attain near-perfect accuracy under i.i.d.\ evaluation, yet deployment security depends on robustness to post-deployment feature manipulation. We study this gap through a cost-aware evasion framework that models discrete, monotone feature edits under explicit attacker budgets. Three diagnostics are introduced: minimal evasion cost (MEC), the evasion survival rate $S(B)$, and the robustness concentration index (RCI).
  On the UCI Phishing Websites benchmark (11\,055 instances, 30 ternary features), Logistic Regression, Random Forests, Gradient Boosted Trees, and XGBoost all achieve $\mathrm{AUC}\ge 0.979$ under static evaluation. Under budgeted sanitization-style evasion, robustness converges across architectures: the median MEC equals 2 with full features, and over 80\% of successful minimal-cost evasions concentrate on three low-cost surface features. Feature restriction improves robustness only when it removes all dominant low-cost transitions. Under strict cost schedules, infrastructure-leaning feature sets exhibit 17-19\% infeasible mass for ensemble models, while the median MEC among evadable instances remains unchanged. We formalize this convergence: if a positive fraction of correctly detected phishing instances admit evasion through a single feature transition of minimal cost $c_{\min}$, no classifier can raise the corresponding MEC quantile above $c_{\min}$ without modifying the feature representation or cost model. Adversarial robustness in phishing detection is governed by feature economics rather than model complexity.

</details>


### [94] [Online Learning and Equilibrium Computation with Ranking Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19221)
*Mingyang Liu,Yongshan Chen,Zhiyuan Fan,Gabriele Farina,Asuman Ozdaglar,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究仅通过排名反馈而非数值效用反馈的在线学习问题，分析了瞬时效用排名和时间平均效用排名两种反馈机制下的遗憾界，提出了在效用序列具有次线性总变差假设下的次线性遗憾算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线学习算法依赖数值效用反馈，但在人机交互应用和隐私保护场景中，数值反馈可能不可用或受限。本文研究仅通过排名反馈进行在线学习的模型，探索在更受限反馈下的学习能力。

Method: 研究两种排名反馈机制：基于瞬时效用的排名和基于时间平均效用的排名，分别在完全信息和赌博机反馈设置下分析。使用外部遗憾度量，在Plackett-Luce模型下分析学习界限，并提出在效用序列具有次线性总变差假设下的新算法。

Result: 1) 在一般情况下，瞬时效用排名反馈无法实现次线性遗憾；2) 在Plackett-Luce模型温度足够小时，时间平均效用排名反馈也无法实现次线性遗憾；3) 在效用序列具有次线性总变差假设下，提出了实现次线性遗憾的新算法；4) 对于完全信息时间平均效用排名反馈，可以不需要额外假设；5) 在正常形式博弈中，所有参与者采用该算法可实现近似粗相关均衡。

Conclusion: 仅通过排名反馈进行在线学习在一般情况下具有挑战性，但在特定条件下（如效用序列变化平缓）可以实现次线性遗憾。该研究为受限反馈环境下的在线学习提供了理论框架，并在大规模语言模型路由任务中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Online learning in arbitrary, and possibly adversarial, environments has been extensively studied in sequential decision-making, and it is closely connected to equilibrium computation in game theory. Most existing online learning algorithms rely on \emph{numeric} utility feedback from the environment, which may be unavailable in human-in-the-loop applications and/or may be restricted by privacy concerns. In this paper, we study an online learning model in which the learner only observes a \emph{ranking} over a set of proposed actions at each timestep. We consider two ranking mechanisms: rankings induced by the \emph{instantaneous} utility at the current timestep, and rankings induced by the \emph{time-average} utility up to the current timestep, under both \emph{full-information} and \emph{bandit} feedback settings. Using the standard external-regret metric, we show that sublinear regret is impossible with instantaneous-utility ranking feedback in general. Moreover, when the ranking model is relatively deterministic, \emph{i.e.}, under the Plackett-Luce model with a temperature that is sufficiently small, sublinear regret is also impossible with time-average utility ranking feedback. We then develop new algorithms that achieve sublinear regret under the additional assumption that the utility sequence has sublinear total variation. Notably, for full-information time-average utility ranking feedback, this additional assumption can be removed. As a consequence, when all players in a normal-form game follow our algorithms, repeated play yields an approximate coarse correlated equilibrium. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of our algorithms in an online large-language-model routing task.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Can LLMs Reason Like Automated Theorem Provers for Rust Verification? VCoT-Bench: Evaluating via Verification Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18334)
*Zichen Xie,Wenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VCoT-Lift框架将底层求解器推理提升为高层可读的验证步骤，创建VCoT-Bench基准评估LLMs在Rust程序验证中的推理能力，发现当前模型存在严重脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将Rust验证视为黑盒，仅通过二元通过/失败结果评估模型，无法判断模型是否真正理解验证非平凡Rust代码所需的逻辑推理过程。

Method: 提出VCoT-Lift框架，将底层求解器推理提升为高层、人类可读的验证步骤，形成显式的验证思维链。基于此构建VCoT-Bench基准，包含1,988个VCoT完成任务，从三个正交维度评估LLMs：对不同程度缺失证明的鲁棒性、对不同证明类型的能力、对证明位置的敏感性。

Result: 评估十个最先进模型显示严重脆弱性，表明当前LLMs远未达到自动定理证明器所展示的推理能力。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够理解完整验证过程的LLMs，VCoT-Lift和VCoT-Bench为细粒度评估提供了基础，揭示了当前模型在Rust程序验证推理能力上的不足。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly assist secure software development, their ability to meet the rigorous demands of Rust program verification remains unclear. Existing evaluations treat Rust verification as a black box, assessing models only by binary pass or fail outcomes for proof hints. This obscures whether models truly understand the logical deductions required for verifying nontrivial Rust code. To bridge this gap, we introduce VCoT-Lift, a framework that lifts low-level solver reasoning into high-level, human-readable verification steps. By exposing solver-level reasoning as an explicit Verification Chain-of-Thought, VCoT-Lift provides a concrete ground truth for fine-grained evaluation. Leveraging VCoT-Lift, we introduce VCoT-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark of 1,988 VCoT completion tasks for rigorously evaluating LLMs' understanding of the entire verification process. VCoT-Bench measures performance along three orthogonal dimensions: robustness to varying degrees of missing proofs, competence across different proof types, and sensitivity to the proof locations. Evaluation of ten state-of-the-art models reveals severe fragility, indicating that current LLMs fall well short of the reasoning capabilities exhibited by automated theorem provers.

</details>


### [96] [Where are the Hidden Gems? Applying Transformer Models for Design Discussion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18393)
*Lawrence Arkoh,Daniel Feitosa,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估了多种Transformer模型（BERT、RoBERTa、XLNet、LaMini-Flan-T5-77M和ChatGPT-4o-mini）在跨域检测设计相关讨论任务中的表现，发现ChatGPT-4o-mini在召回率方面表现最佳，而BERT和RoBERTa在跨域泛化方面表现稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中的设计决策信息分散在各种讨论平台中，机器学习方法可用于检测这些设计讨论，但面临标注数据稀缺和标注成本高的问题。先前研究主要使用传统分类器的跨域策略，而性能更优的Transformer模型在此场景下尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 对先前跨域研究进行概念性复现，并扩展使用现代Transformer架构（BERT、RoBERTa、XLNet、LaMini-Flan-T5-77M和ChatGPT-4o-mini）。模型在Stack Overflow数据上微调，在GitHub工件（pull requests、issues、commits）上评估。同时评估了相似词注入的数据增强方法。

Result: BERT和RoBERTa在跨域场景下表现出较强的召回率；XLNet实现了更高的精确率但召回率较低；ChatGPT-4o-mini获得了最高的召回率和有竞争力的整体性能；LaMini-Flan-T5-77M作为轻量级替代方案，提供了更强的精确率但性能不够均衡。相似词注入的数据增强方法未带来有意义的改进。

Conclusion: 现代语言模型在检测设计讨论方面既有机遇也有权衡，不同模型在精确率和召回率之间存在不同的平衡点。研究结果强调了在实际应用中需要根据具体需求选择合适的模型架构。

Abstract: Design decisions are at the core of software engineering and appear in Q\&A forums, mailing lists, pull requests, issue trackers, and commit messages. Design discussions spanning a project's history provide valuable information for informed decision-making, such as refactoring and software modernization. Machine learning techniques have been used to detect design decisions in natural language discussions; however, their effectiveness is limited by the scarcity of labeled data and the high cost of annotation. Prior work adopted cross-domain strategies with traditional classifiers, training on one domain and testing on another. Despite their success, transformer-based models, which often outperform traditional methods, remain largely unexplored in this setting. The goal of this work is to investigate the performance of transformer-based models (i.e., BERT, RoBERTa, XLNet, LaMini-Flan-T5-77M, and ChatGPT-4o-mini) for detecting design-related discussions. To this end, we conduct a conceptual replication of prior cross-domain studies while extending them with modern transformer architectures and addressing methodological issues in earlier work. The models were fine-tuned on Stack Overflow and evaluated on GitHub artifacts (i.e., pull requests, issues, and commits). BERT and RoBERTa show strong recall across domains, while XLNet achieves higher precision but lower recall. ChatGPT-4o-mini yields the highest recall and competitive overall performance, whereas LaMini-Flan-T5-77M provides a lightweight alternative with stronger precision but less balanced performance. We also evaluated similar-word injection for data augmentation, but unlike prior findings, it did not yield meaningful improvements. Overall, these results highlight both the opportunities and trade-offs of using modern language models for detecting design discussion.

</details>


### [97] [SQL-Commenter: Aligning Large Language Models for SQL Comment Generation with Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18606)
*Lei Yu,Peng Wang,Jingyuan Zhang,Xin Wang,Jia Xu,Li Yang,Changzhi Deng,Jiajia Ma,Fengjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SQL-Commenter：基于LLaMA-3.1-8B的SQL注释生成方法，通过持续预训练、监督微调和直接偏好优化，显著提升复杂SQL查询的注释质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQL查询理解面临复杂语法、多样连接类型和深度嵌套的挑战，缺乏充分注释严重影响代码可读性、可维护性和知识传递。现有方法存在两个主要问题：数据集不足以代表复杂真实查询，以及大语言模型对SQL特定语义理解不足

Method: 1. 构建包含复杂SQL查询和专家验证注释的综合数据集；2. 在大规模SQL语料上进行持续预训练以增强语法和语义理解；3. 进行监督微调；4. 引入基于人类反馈的直接偏好优化（DPO），使用偏好损失函数优化模型输出

Result: 在Spider和Bird基准测试中，SQL-Commenter显著优于最先进基线。平均而言，在BLEU-4、METEOR和ROUGE-L指标上分别超过最强基线（Qwen3-14B）9.29、4.99和13.23个百分点。人工评估显示生成的注释在正确性、完整性和自然性方面具有优越质量

Conclusion: SQL-Commenter通过综合数据集构建、持续预训练、监督微调和直接偏好优化的组合方法，有效解决了SQL注释生成的挑战，显著提升了复杂SQL查询的注释质量，为SQL代码理解和维护提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: SQL query comprehension is a significant challenge due to complex syntax, diverse join types, and deep nesting. Many queries lack adequate comments, severely hindering code readability, maintainability, and knowledge transfer. Automated SQL comment generation faces two main challenges: limited datasets that inadequately represent complex real-world queries, and Large Language Models' (LLMs) insufficient understanding of SQL-specific semantics. Our empirical analysis shows that even after continual pre-training and supervised fine-tuning, LLMs struggle with complex SQL semantics, yielding inaccurate comments. To address this, we propose SQL-Commenter, an advanced method based on LLaMA-3.1-8B. We first construct a comprehensive dataset of complex SQL queries with expert-verified comments. Next, we perform continual pre-training on a large SQL corpus to enhance the LLM's syntax and semantic understanding, followed by supervised fine-tuning. Finally, we introduce Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) using human feedback. SQL-Commenter utilizes a preference-based loss function to favor preferred outputs, enhancing fine-grained semantic learning and context-dependent quality assessment. Evaluated on the Spider and Bird benchmarks, SQL-Commenter significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. On average, it surpasses the strongest baseline (Qwen3-14B) by 9.29, 4.99, and 13.23 percentage points on BLEU-4, METEOR, and ROUGE-L, respectively. Moreover, human evaluation demonstrates the superior quality of comments generated by SQL-Commenter in terms of correctness, completeness, and naturalness.

</details>


### [98] [Green Architectural Tactics in ML-enabled Systems: An LLM-based Repository Mining Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18734)
*Vincenzo De Martino,Silverio Martínez-Fernández,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析205个GitHub开源ML项目，调查了绿色AI实践在实际中的采用情况，并利用LLM方法发现了9种新的可持续实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习和人工智能技术的广泛应用，其环境可持续性问题日益受到关注。ML系统的开发和部署（特别是训练和推理阶段）计算密集，能耗巨大。虽然已有研究提出了绿色AI实践目录，但缺乏对这些实践在实际中采用程度的了解，也不清楚是否存在未记录的可持续实践。

Method: 采用软件仓库挖掘研究方法，分析205个GitHub开源ML项目。设计了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的新机制，能够从代码仓库中识别已知和新的可持续实践。

Result: 研究发现文献中报告的绿色策略确实在实践中被使用，但采用率存在差异。更重要的是，基于LLM的方法揭示了9种先前未记录的可持续实践。每种策略都提供了代码示例以支持采用和集成。

Conclusion: 为寻求减少ML系统环境影响的从业者提供了实用见解，并为未来自动化检测和采用可持续实践的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Context: The increasing adoption of machine learning (ML) and artificial intelligence (AI) technologies raises growing concerns about their environmental sustainability. Developing and deploying ML-enabled systems is computationally intensive, particularly during training and inference. Green AI has emerged to address these issues by promoting efficiency without sacrificing accuracy. While prior research has proposed catalogs of sustainable practices (i.e., green tactics), there remains limited understanding of their adoption in practice and whether additional, undocumented tactics exist. Objective: This study aims to investigate the extent to which existing sustainable practices are implemented in real-world ML-enabled systems and to identify previously undocumented practices that support environmental sustainability. Method: We conduct a mining software repository study on 205 open-source ML projects on GitHub. To support our analysis, we design a novel mechanism based on large language models (LLMs) capable of identifying both known and new sustainable practices from code repositories. Results: Our findings confirm that green tactics reported in the literature are used in practice, although adoption rates vary. Furthermore, our LLM-based approach reveals nine previously undocumented sustainable practices. Each tactic is supported with code examples to aid adoption and integration. Conclusions: We finally provide insights for practitioners seeking to reduce the environmental impact of ML-enabled systems and offer a foundation for future research in automating the detection and adoption of sustainable practices.

</details>


### [99] [SpaceTime Programming: Live and Omniscient Exploration of Code and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18735)
*Jean-Baptiste Döderlein,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher,Benoit Combemale*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpaceTime Programming统一了探索性编程、实时编程和全知调试，通过追踪代码变更和执行状态，让开发者能在空间（代码变体）和时间（执行流程）两个维度探索程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统编程环境将静态代码与动态执行分离，开发者需要在编写代码和观察执行之间切换，缺乏理解代码变更与运行时行为关系的工具。现有解决方案往往只关注特定方面或单一范式，缺乏集成多种能力的完整环境。

Method: 提出SpaceTime Programming方法，核心是追踪机制，不仅捕获执行状态，还记录对应的代码变更。作为概念验证，实现了Python库SpaceTimePy，支持两种应用：实时全知调试器和Pygame游戏开发工具。

Result: 在五个真实Python项目上评估SpaceTimePy，测试套件的性能开销在35%到150%之间。通过Flappy Bird类游戏展示了Pygame工具的应用。

Conclusion: SpaceTime Programming统一了多种编程范式，为探索代码修改和执行流程提供了新的编程模型，通过追踪机制实现了空间和时间维度的程序探索。

Abstract: Programming environments typically separate the world of static code from the dynamic execution of programs. Developers must switch between writing code and observing its execution, often with limited tools to understand the relationship between code changes and runtime behavior. Several paradigms and approaches exist to bridge this gap, including exploratory programming for comparing code variants, live programming for immediate feedback, and omniscient debugging for exploring execution history. However, existing solutions tend to focus on specific aspects and one specific paradigm rather than providing a fully integrated environment with multiple capabilities. This paper introduces \spacetime Programming, a novel approach that unifies these paradigms to create a programming model for exploring both code modifications and execution flow. At the core of our approach is a trace mechanism that captures not only execution state but also the corresponding code changes, enabling developers to explore programs in both space (code variants) and time (execution flow). As a proof of concept, we implemented a Python library supporting SpaceTime Programming and applied it in two contexts: a live omniscient debugger and a Pygame game development tool, showcased through a Flappy Bird-like game. We further evaluated SpaceTimePy on five real-world Python projects, finding performance overhead ranging from 35% to 150% on test suites.

</details>


### [100] [Measuring and Exploiting Confirmation Bias in LLM-Assisted Security Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18740)
*Dimitris Mitropoulos,Nikolaos Alexopoulos,Georgios Alexopoulos,Diomidis Spinellis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM代码审查存在确认偏误漏洞，可通过恶意PR元数据操纵降低漏洞检测率，去偏方法可恢复检测能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究确认偏误是否影响基于LLM的漏洞检测，以及这种缺陷是否可在软件供应链攻击中被利用

Method: 采用两项互补研究：研究1通过250个CVE漏洞/补丁对在四种先进模型和五种提示框架条件下量化确认偏误；研究2模拟对抗性PR，通过元数据框架作为安全改进或紧急功能修复来重新引入已知漏洞

Result: 研究1：将变更框架为无缺陷时，漏洞检测率降低16-93%，假阴性显著增加而假阳性变化不大；研究2：对抗性框架在GitHub Copilot中成功率为35%（单次攻击），在Claude Code中达88%（迭代优化）；去偏方法可恢复所有交互式案例和94%自主案例的检测

Conclusion: 确认偏误构成LLM代码审查的弱点，影响AI辅助开发工具的部署方式，需要采取去偏措施如元数据删除和明确指令

Abstract: Security code reviews increasingly rely on systems integrating Large Language Models (LLMs), ranging from interactive assistants to autonomous agents in CI/CD pipelines. We study whether confirmation bias (i.e., the tendency to favor interpretations that align with prior expectations) affects LLM-based vulnerability detection, and whether this failure mode can be exploited in software supply-chain attacks. We conduct two complementary studies.
  Study 1 quantifies confirmation bias through controlled experiments on 250 CVE vulnerability/patch pairs evaluated across four state-of-the-art models under five framing conditions for the review prompt. Framing a change as bug-free reduces vulnerability detection rates by 16-93%, with strongly asymmetric effects: false negatives increase sharply while false positive rates change little. Bias effects vary by vulnerability type, with injection flaws being more susceptible to them than memory corruption bugs.
  Study 2 evaluates exploitability in practice mimicking adversarial pull requests that reintroduce known vulnerabilities while framed as security improvements or urgent functionality fixes via their pull request metadata. Adversarial framing succeeds in 35% of cases against GitHub Copilot (interactive assistant) under one-shot attacks and in 88% of cases against Claude Code (autonomous agent) in real project configurations where adversaries can iteratively refine their framing to increase attack success. Debiasing via metadata redaction and explicit instructions restores detection in all interactive cases and 94% of autonomous cases. Our results show that confirmation bias poses a weakness in LLM-based code review, with implications on how AI-assisted development tools are deployed.

</details>

### [101] [A vision for a colorectal digital twin that enables proactive and personalized disease management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18064)
*Sayed Chhattan Shah,Andrea Townsend-Nicholson,Spiros Denaxas,Pablo Lamata,Manish Chand*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出结直肠数字孪生概念框架，用于非侵入性连续监测和个性化疾病管理，整合多模态数据、混合建模和AI引擎


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠癌、炎症性肠病和憩室病等疾病影响全球数百万人，造成巨大临床和经济负担。当前基于偶发临床就诊、实验室检测和被动干预的护理路径限制了早期检测和个性化纵向管理

Method: 提出结直肠数字孪生概念框架，整合多模态生理和行为数据流、结直肠功能的混合机理-机器学习建模、个性化人工智能引擎，支持主动疾病管理

Result: 本文未呈现已部署的临床系统，而是提出了结直肠数字孪生的清晰愿景和结构化方法，确定了未来实施和验证所需的关键技术、建模和转化挑战

Conclusion: 结直肠数字孪生框架为早期检测和个性化疾病管理提供了有前景的解决方案，但需要解决技术、建模和转化方面的挑战才能实现临床应用

Abstract: Colorectal cancer, inflammatory bowel disease, and diverticular disease are progressive conditions that affect millions of individuals worldwide and impose substantial clinical and economic burdens. Early detection and personalized management are essential for slowing disease progression and improving patient outcomes. Current care pathways rely primarily on episodic clinical encounters, laboratory testing, and reactive interventions, limiting early detection and personalized longitudinal management. This paper introduces a conceptual framework for an integrated colorectal digital twin that supports non-invasive, continuous monitoring and personalized disease management. The framework integrates multimodal physiological and behavioral data streams, hybrid mechanistic-machine learning modeling of colorectal function, and a personalized artificial intelligence engine to support proactive disease management. Rather than presenting a deployed clinical system, this work outlines a clear vision and a structured approach for colorectal digital twins, identifying key technical, modeling, and translational challenges necessary for future implementation and validation.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [A Trace-Based Assurance Framework for Agentic AI Orchestration: Contracts, Testing, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18096)
*Ciprian Paduraru,Petru-Liviu Bouruc,Alin Stefanescu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于Agentic AI系统的保障框架，通过消息-动作追踪、契约验证、压力测试和运行时治理来确保多智能体LLM系统的可靠性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统中，LLMs在编排层协调多个智能体并与外部服务交互时，会出现非终止、角色漂移、错误传播等复杂故障，需要系统化的保障机制来确保可靠性和安全性。

Method: 1) 将执行过程建模为带显式步骤和追踪契约的消息-动作追踪；2) 压力测试作为有界扰动下的预算化反例搜索；3) 在服务、检索和内存边界进行结构化故障注入；4) 将治理作为运行时组件，实施能力限制和动作仲裁。

Result: 定义了基于追踪的度量标准，包括任务成功率、终止可靠性、契约合规性、事实性指标、遏制率和治理结果分布，支持跨随机种子、模型和编排配置的比较评估。

Conclusion: 该框架为多智能体LLM系统的测试和评估提供了通用抽象，促进了不同编排设计和配置之间的可重复比较，有助于提升Agentic AI系统的可靠性和安全性保障。

Abstract: In Agentic AI, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in the orchestration layer to coordinate multiple agents and to interact with external services, retrieval components, and shared memory. In this setting, failures are not limited to incorrect final outputs. They also arise from long-horizon interaction, stochastic decisions, and external side effects (such as API calls, database writes, and message sends). Common failures include non-termination, role drift, propagation of unsupported claims, and attacks via untrusted context or external channels.
  This paper presents an assurance framework for such Agentic AI systems. Executions are instrumented as Message-Action Traces (MAT) with explicit step and trace contracts. Contracts provide machine-checkable verdicts, localize the first violating step, and support deterministic replay. The framework includes stress testing, formulated as a budgeted counterexample search over bounded perturbations. It also supports structured fault injection at service, retrieval, and memory boundaries to assess containment under realistic operational faults and degraded conditions. Finally, governance is treated as a runtime component, enforcing per-agent capability limits and action mediation (allow, rewrite, block) at the language-to-action boundary.
  To support comparative evaluations across stochastic seeds, models, and orchestration configurations, the paper defines trace-based metrics for task success, termination reliability, contract compliance, factuality indicators, containment rate, and governance outcome distributions. More broadly, the framework is intended as a common abstraction to support testing and evaluation of multi-agent LLM systems, and to facilitate reproducible comparison across orchestration designs and configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Conditional Execution of Transpiler Passes Based on Per-Script Feature Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18049)
*Rishipal Singh Bhatia*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于特征集动态跟踪的JavaScript编译器优化方法，通过条件执行转译通道减少不必要的AST遍历，显著提升编译性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级JavaScript编译器面临支持现代语法同时保持向后兼容的挑战。传统编译器基于目标语言级别严格选择转译通道，导致大量计算浪费，因为编译器会执行昂贵的AST遍历来降低实际输入源代码中可能不存在的语言特性。

Method: 提出编译器改进方案：基于准确跟踪和动态维护编译单元中实际存在的语言特性集合，条件执行转译通道。在Google Closure Compiler中实现，通过在JavaScript脚本级别填充和维护FeatureSet，动态跳过不必要的降低通道。包括策略性通道排序和动态验证转译代码的特征正确性等架构保障措施。

Result: 在大规模生产级monorepos上的评估显示，该改进显著减少了编译时间，并节省了计算和内存使用。

Conclusion: 通过动态跟踪语言特性集合并条件执行转译通道，可以有效优化JavaScript编译器的性能，减少不必要的计算开销，提高编译效率。

Abstract: As the ECMAScript specification evolves, industrial-scale JavaScript compilers face the challenge of supporting modern language syntax while maintaining compatibility for diverse execution environments. Traditionally, compilers solve this by running transpilation passes in a monolithic pipeline, where the transpilation passes are chosen to execute strictly based on a target language level. This results in significant computational waste, as compilers perform expensive Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) traversals to lower features that may not exist in the actual input source code. We present a compiler improvement that conditionally executes transpiler passes based on accurately tracking and dynamically maintaining the exact set of language features present in the compilation unit throughout the transpilation process. It is implemented in the production Google Closure Compiler. By populating and maintaining a FeatureSet at every JavaScript script-level, it dynamically skips running unnecessary lowering passes. We detail the architectural safeguards -- including strategic pass ordering and dynamic validation of the transpiled code for feature-correctness. Evaluation of this improvement on large-scale production monorepos produced a considerable reduction in compilation time and saved compute and memory usage.

</details>


### [104] [TENSURE: Fuzzing Sparse Tensor Compilers (Registered Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18372)
*Kabilan Mahathevan,Yining Zhang,Muhammad Ali Gulzar,Kirshanthan Sundararajah*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: TENSURE：首个专为稀疏张量编译器设计的可扩展黑盒模糊测试框架，使用爱因斯坦求和符号作为输入抽象，通过约束生成算法保证100%语义有效性，在TACO和Finch系统中发现了广泛缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏张量编译器（STCs）需要从高级声明式规范合成复杂、不规则的控制流，极易出现细微的正确性缺陷。现有测试框架无法有效测试这些编译器的任意循环合成能力，而通用基于语法的模糊测试器难以生成有效输入。

Method: 提出TENSURE框架：1）使用爱因斯坦求和符号作为通用输入抽象，生成复杂非常规张量收缩；2）设计新颖的基于约束的生成算法，保证合成内核100%语义有效性；3）引入语义保持的变异算子，利用代数交换性和存储格式异构性进行蜕变测试。

Result: 在TACO和Finch两个最先进系统上的评估显示广泛脆弱性：TENSURE在TACO中暴露了大多数生成测试用例的崩溃或静默错误编译，显著优于基线语法模糊测试器约3.3%的有效率。

Conclusion: 稀疏编译生态系统迫切需要专门的测试工具，TENSURE作为首个专为STCs设计的黑盒模糊测试框架，能够有效发现代码生成阶段的边界情况和缺陷。

Abstract: Sparse Tensor Compilers (STCs) have emerged as critical infrastructure for optimizing high-dimensional data analytics and machine learning workloads. The STCs must synthesize complex, irregular control flow for various compressed storage formats directly from high-level declarative specifications, thereby making them highly susceptible to subtle correctness defects. Existing testing frameworks, which rely on mutating computation graphs restricted to a standard vocabulary of operators, fail to exercise the arbitrary loop synthesis capabilities of these compilers. Furthermore, generic grammar-based fuzzers struggle to generate valid inputs due to the strict rules governing how indices are reused across multiple tensors.
  In this paper, we present TENSURE, the first extensible black-box fuzzing framework specifically designed for the testing of STCs. TENSURE leverages Einstein Summation (Einsum) notation as a general input abstraction, enabling the generation of complex, unconventional tensor contractions that expose corner cases in the code-generation phases of STCs. We propose a novel constraint-based generation algorithm that guarantees 100% semantic validity of synthesized kernels, significantly outperforming the ~3.3% validity rate of baseline grammar fuzzers. To enable metamorphic testing without a trusted reference, we introduce a set of semantic-preserving mutation operators that exploit algebraic commutativity and heterogeneity in storage formats. Our evaluation on two state-of-the-art systems, TACO and Finch, reveals widespread fragility, particularly in TACO, where TENSURE exposed crashes or silent miscompilations in a majority of generated test cases. These findings underscore the critical need for specialized testing tools in the sparse compilation ecosystem.

</details>


### [105] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Generalizing Peephole Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18477)
*Chunhao Liao,Hongxu Xu,Xintong Zhou,Zhenyang Xu,Chengnian Sun*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: LPG使用大语言模型来自动化窥孔优化的泛化过程，相比现有方法显著提升了泛化能力和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有窥孔优化泛化方法（如Hydra）存在搜索空间爆炸、泛化不足、对多样化指令域支持有限等问题，需要大量人工努力和领域专业知识。LLMs在语义抽象和探索性推理方面表现出色，但需要形式分析来确保规则的正确性和收益性。

Method: LPG采用闭环工作流，集成LLM驱动的符号常量泛化、结构泛化、约束松弛和位宽/精度泛化，并通过语法验证、语义验证和收益性检查提供反馈。

Result: 在LLVM生态系统的真实窥孔优化问题上，LPG成功泛化了102个优化中的90个。在与Hydra直接可比的整数优化子集上，LPG泛化了81个中的74个，而Hydra仅泛化了35个。

Conclusion: LPG框架通过结合LLMs的语义抽象能力和形式分析的严谨性，显著提升了窥孔优化泛化的自动化程度和效果，为编译器优化提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: Peephole optimizations are a core component of modern optimizing compilers. It rewrites specific instruction into semantically equivalent but more efficient forms. In practice, creating a new peephole optimization often starts from a concrete optimization instance and requires lifting it into a more general rewrite rule that matches a wider range of instruction patterns. This generalization step is critical to optimization effectiveness, but it is also difficult: producing rules that are both correct and sufficiently general typically demands substantial manual effort and domain expertise. Existing approaches such as Hydra attempt to automate this task with program synthesis, but their generalization capability is often limited by search-space explosion, under-generalization, and restricted support for diverse instruction domains.
  We present LPG, large language model aided peephole optimization generalization, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to generalize peephole optimizations. The design of LPG is motivated by the observation that LLMs are effective at semantic abstraction and exploratory reasoning, while formal analyses are necessary to ensure that generated rules are sound and profitable. Based on this observation, LPG adopts a closed-loop workflow that integrates LLM-driven symbolic constant generalization, structural generalization, constraint relaxation, and bitwidth/precision generalization with feedback from syntactic validation, semantic verification, and profitability checking.
  We evaluate LPG on real-world peephole optimization issues drawn from the LLVM ecosystem. Overall, LPG successfully generalizes 90 out of 102 optimizations. On the integer-focused subset that is directly comparable to Hydra, LPG generalizes 74 out of 81 optimizations, whereas Hydra generalizes 35.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [QuaQue: Design and SQL Implementation of Condensed Algebra for Concurrent Versioning of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18654)
*Jey Puget Gil,Emmanuel Coquery,John Samuel,Gilles Gesquière*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: QuaQue系统通过将SPARQL查询转换为SQL，利用压缩代数在关系数据库中高效查询多版本知识图谱


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 版本化知识图谱的管理面临重大挑战，特别是在跨多个版本高效查询数据方面。现有方法在处理并发版本查询时效率不足。

Method: QuaQue作为ConVer-G系统的关键组件，采用新颖的压缩代数操作关系模型，其中版本信息使用位串紧凑存储。该方法将SPARQL代数转换为SQL，在标准关系数据库系统中实现高效查询。

Result: 通过对比基准测试，展示了该方法相对于原生RDF三元组存储的可行性和性能优势。

Conclusion: QuaQue系统提供了一种在关系数据库中高效查询多版本知识图谱的有效方法，通过压缩代数和SPARQL到SQL的转换实现了性能提升。

Abstract: The management of versioned knowledge graphs presents significant challenges, particularly in querying data across multiple versions efficiently. This paper introduces QuaQue, a key component of the ConVer-G system, which addresses this challenge by translating SPARQL (SPARQL Protocol and RDF Query Language) queries into SQL (Structured Query Language). QuaQue leverages a novel condensed algebra to operate on a relational model where versioning information is compactly stored using bitstrings. This approach allows for efficient querying of concurrent versions of knowledge graphs within a standard relational database system. We present the key concepts of our condensed algebra, detail the translation process from SPARQL algebra to SQL, and provide a comparative benchmark against a native RDF (Resource Description Framework) triple store, demonstrating the viability and performance benefits of our approach.

</details>


### [107] [Let's Play Tag: Linear Time Evaluation of Conjunctive Queries under TGD Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18709)
*Nofar Carmeli,Carsten Lutz,Marcin Przybyłko*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 研究在元组生成依赖约束下合取查询线性时间评估的极限，探索不同评估模式下的分类可能性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在数据库约束（TGDs）下合取查询评估的复杂性分类，特别是在线性时间评估的可行性方面，填补无约束设置与约束设置之间的知识空白

Method: 提出一种方法，将无约束设置中的已知二分法提升到某些TGD类约束下的查询评估，特别关注非递归TGDs和边界保护全TGDs两类约束

Result: 方法适用于除枚举外的所有评估模式（单测试、全测试、计数、字典序直接访问），当约束属于两类TGDs时：1）头部最多使用二元关系符号或最多两个边界变量的非递归TGDs；2）边界保护全TGDs

Conclusion: 虽然完全分类似乎遥不可及，但提出的方法能够将无约束设置中的二分法扩展到某些TGD类约束下的查询评估，同时通过示例展示了枚举和更宽松TGD类带来的挑战

Abstract: We study the limits of linear time evaluation of conjunctive queries under constraints expressed as tuple-generating dependencies (TGDs), across several modes of query evaluation: single-testing, all-testing, counting, lexicographic direct access, and enumeration. While full classifications seem far beyond reach, we propose an approach that, for some evaluation modes and classes of TGDs, makes it possible to lift known dichotomies from the unconstrained setting. In particular, our approach applies to all mentioned evaluation modes except enumeration, when the constraints fall into one of two classes: non-recursive sets of TGDs in which every TGD uses at most binary relation symbols in the head or has at most two frontier variables; and frontier-guarded full TGDs. We further provide a collection of examples showcasing the challenges that arise for enumeration and for less restrictive classes of TGDs.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Auditing Preferences for Brands and Cultures in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18300)
*Jasmine Rienecker,Katarina Mpofu,Naman Goel,Siddhartha Datta,Jun Zhao,Oscar Danielsson,Fredrik Thorsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ChoiceEval是一个用于审计大型语言模型在品牌和文化偏好方面系统性风险的框架，通过生成多样化的用户画像查询，将自由格式输出转换为可比较的选择集，量化模型的地理偏好和偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的AI系统在数十亿人的选择、购买和信息获取中扮演中介角色，迫切需要量化LLM驱动市场中介的系统性风险，包括对市场公平性、竞争和信息多样性的影响。

Method: ChoiceEval框架通过两个核心技术解决：1)生成基于心理画像的多样化评估查询，将用户分为预算敏感型、健康导向型、便利型等画像；2)将自由格式输出转换为标准化的top-k选择集，并使用可比较的指标量化偏好和地理偏见。

Result: 在Gemini、GPT和DeepSeek三个模型上应用ChoiceEval，涵盖10个商业和文化主题的2000多个问题，结果显示：美国开发的Gemini和GPT对美国实体表现出明显偏好，而中国开发的DeepSeek显示出更平衡但仍可检测的地理偏好，这些模式在不同用户画像中持续存在。

Conclusion: ChoiceEval提供了一个可扩展的审计管道，将模型行为与现实经济结果联系起来，揭示了LLM中存在的系统性偏好模式，这对研究人员、平台和监管机构评估和缓解模型偏见具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) based AI systems increasingly mediate what billions of people see, choose and buy. This creates an urgent need to quantify the systemic risks of LLM-driven market intermediation, including its implications for market fairness, competition, and the diversity of information exposure.
  This paper introduces ChoiceEval, a reproducible framework for auditing preferences for brands and cultures in large language models (LLMs) under realistic usage conditions. ChoiceEval addresses two core technical challenges: (i) generating realistic, persona-diverse evaluation queries and (ii) converting free-form outputs into comparable choice sets and quantitative preference metrics. For a given topic (e.g. running shoes, hotel chains, travel destinations), the framework segments users into psychographic profiles (e.g., budget-conscious, wellness-focused, convenience), and then derives diverse prompts that reflect real-world advice-seeking and decision-making behaviour. LLM responses are converted into normalised top-k choice sets. Preference and geographic bias are then quantified using comparable metrics across topics and personas. Thus, ChoiceEval provides a scalable audit pipeline for researchers, platforms, and regulators, linking model behaviour to real-world economic outcomes.
  Applied to Gemini, GPT, and DeepSeek across 10 topics spanning commerce and culture and more than 2,000 questions, ChoiceEval reveals consistent preferences: U.S.-developed models Gemini and GPT show marked favouritism toward American entities, while China-developed DeepSeek exhibits more balanced yet still detectable geographic preferences. These patterns persist across user personas, suggesting systematic rather than incidental effects.

</details>


### [109] [PeriphAR: Fast and Accurate Real-World Object Selection with Peripheral Augmented Reality Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18350)
*Yutong Ren,Arnav Reddy,Michael Nebeling*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PeriphAR是一种利用周边视觉进行注视选择反馈的可视化技术，针对单目AR显示器设计，通过增强目标颜色对比度来优化周边视觉的感知效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XR中的注视选择需要视觉确认，而现有的世界锁定中央覆盖设计不适合始终开启的AR眼镜。需要一种能够利用周边视觉进行反馈的技术，以支持更自然的AR交互。

Method: 1) 首次用户研究：分离文本、颜色和形状属性，比较周边选择线索；2) 开发两种增强周边视觉颜色的策略；3) 第二次用户研究：评估策略的主观偏好；4) 实现端到端系统，结合真实世界物体检测进行概念验证。

Result: 周边视觉对颜色比对形状更敏感，但在低对比度下敏感性迅速下降。最大化目标与最相似颜色相邻物体对比度的策略主观上更受偏好。PeriphAR系统成功实现了基于真实世界物体检测的注视选择。

Conclusion: PeriphAR通过利用周边视觉进行注视选择反馈，为AR眼镜提供了一种有效的可视化技术。通过增强颜色对比度的策略，可以优化周边视觉的感知效果，支持更自然的AR交互体验。

Abstract: Gaze-based selection in XR requires visual confirmation due to eye-tracking limitations and target ambiguity in 3D contexts. Current designs for wide-FOV displays use world-locked, central overlays, which are not conducive to always-on AR glasses. This paper introduces PeriphAR (per-ree-far), a visualization technique that leverages peripheral vision for feedback during gaze-based selection on a monocular AR display. In a first user study, we isolated text, color, and shape properties of target objects to compare peripheral selection cues. Peripheral vision was more sensitive to color than shape, but this sensitivity rapidly declined at lower contrast. To preserve preattentive processing of color, we developed two strategies to enhance color in users' peripheral vision. In a second user study, our strategy that maximized contrast of the target to the neighboring object with the most similar color was subjectively preferred. As proof of concept, we implemented PeriphAR in an end-to-end system to test performance with real-world object detection.

</details>


### [110] [Deconstructing Open-World Game Mission Design Formula: A Thematic Analysis Using an Action-Block Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18398)
*Kaijie Xu,Yiwei Zhang,Brian Yang,Clark Verbrugge*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出MAQV六维框架和行动块语法，通过LLM解析2200个AAA游戏任务，开发交互式仪表板分析任务设计模式，验证工具可用性并识别设计权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界任务设计常依赖重复公式，但设计师缺乏系统方法来分析大规模任务组合中的节奏、变化和体验平衡

Method: 引入任务行动质量向量（MAQV）六维框架（战斗、探索、叙事、情感、问题解决、独特性）和行动块语法；使用LLM辅助解析将社区攻略转换为结构化行动序列；从20款AAA游戏中收集约2200个任务进行分析；开发交互式仪表板；通过混合方法研究验证工具

Result: 成功解析2200个任务并应用MAQV评分；验证了流程的保真度和工具的可用性；通过主题分析识别出重复的设计权衡、节奏语法以及任务类型和系列演变中的系统性差异

Conclusion: 提供了可重复的分析工作流程、数据驱动的可视化工具和反思性见解，支持大规模、更平衡和多样化的任务设计

Abstract: Open-world missions often rely on repeated formulas, yet designers lack systematic ways to examine pacing, variation, and experiential balance across large portfolios. We introduce the Mission Action Quality Vector (MAQV), a six-dimensional framework-covering combat, exploration, narrative, emotion, problem-solving, and uniqueness-paired with an action block grammar representing missions as gameplay sequences. Using about 2200 missions from 20 AAA titles, we apply LLM-assisted parsing to convert community walkthroughs into structured action sequences and score them with MAQV. An interactive dashboard enables designers to reveal underlying mission formulas. In a mixed-methods study with experienced players and designers, we validate the pipeline's fidelity and the tool's usability, and use thematic analysis to identify recurring design trade-offs, pacing grammars, and systematic differences by quest type and franchise evolution. Our work offers a reproducible analytical workflow, a data-driven visualization tool, and reflective insights to support more balanced, varied mission design at scale.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Ray-Casting: Evaluating Controller, Free-Hand, and Virtual-Touch Modalities for Immersive Text Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18435)
*Md. Tanvir Hossain,Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Akif Islam,Md. Omar Faruqe,Mahboob Qaosar,A. F. M. Mahbubur Rahman,Sanjoy Kumar Chakravarty,M. Khademul Islam Molla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VR文本输入效率对比研究：控制器驱动的手势组合输入在速度和准确性上表现最佳，但虚拟触摸打字在主观体验上评分最高，揭示了效率与自然交互之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本输入效率是阻碍VR成为生产力平台的主要瓶颈，需要实证比较不同物理输入系统以找到最优解决方案。

Method: 对六种物理输入系统进行实证比较，涵盖三种交互风格（控制器驱动、自由手、虚拟触摸），评估离散点击打字和连续手势打字（滑动），并包含语音转文本作为非物理参考模态，共21名参与者参与实验。

Result: 控制器驱动的手势组合输入（CD TGC）表现最佳，速度比最慢系统快2.25倍，比行业标准快30%，错误率降低68%；虚拟触摸点击打字在主观体验评分最高，SUS得分比最低系统高80%；自由手交互受限于跟踪稳定性和身体疲劳，语音输入存在隐私、编辑控制和沉浸感等实际问题。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了VR文本输入中吞吐量与自然交互之间的张力，为未来VR界面设计提供了数据驱动的指导，控制器驱动的手势组合在性能上最优，但虚拟触摸打字在用户体验上更受欢迎。

Abstract: Efficient text entry remains a primary bottleneck preventing Virtual Reality (VR) from evolving into a viable productivity platform. To address this, we conducted an empirical comparison of six physical input systems across three interaction styles Controller Driven, Free Hand, and Virtual Touch evaluating both discrete tap typing and continuous gesture typing (swiping), alongside a speech to text (Voice) condition as a non physical reference modality. Results from 21 participants show that the Controller Driven Tap Gesture Combo (CD TGC) delivers the best productivity performance, achieving speeds 2.25 times higher than the slowest system and 30% faster than the current industry standard, while reducing error rates by up to 68%. A clear trade off emerged between performance and perceived usability: although controller based gesture input led on speed and accuracy, participants rated Virtual Touch Tap Typing highest in subjective experience, scoring 80% higher on the System Usability Scale (SUS) than the lowest rated alternative. We further observe that Free Hand interaction remains limited by tracking stability and physical fatigue, whereas Voice input introduces practical constraints related to privacy, editing control, and immersive engagement. Together, these findings characterize the tension between throughput and natural interaction in immersive text entry and provide data driven guidance for future VR interface design.

</details>


### [112] [Align-to-Scale: Mode Switching Technique for Unimanual 3D Object Manipulation with Gaze-Hand-Object Alignment in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18535)
*Min-yung Kim,Jinwook Kim,Ken Pfeuffer,Sang Ho Yoon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出利用注视与手部空间对齐作为模式切换，实现单手捏合缩放，使XR交互更灵活、移动和可访问


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着XR技术普及，支持单手交互变得至关重要。当前主流的"注视+捏合"交互模型部分支持单手操作，但缩放通常需要双手，限制了交互的灵活性和移动性

Method: 利用注视与手部的空间对齐作为模式切换机制，设计并评估多种单手缩放技术，在复合平移-缩放任务中测试其可用性

Result: 所有提出的方法都能有效实现单手缩放，但每种方法都有独特的优势和权衡。研究发现了不同技术的特点和适用场景

Conclusion: 通过分析不同方法的优缺点，推导出设计指南以支持未来3D界面的单手交互，使XR中的眼手交互更加移动、灵活和可访问

Abstract: As extended reality (XR) technologies rapidly become as ubiquitous as today's mobile devices, supporting one-handed interaction becomes essential for XR. However, the prevalent Gaze + Pinch interaction model partially supports unimanual interaction, where users select, move, and rotate objects with one hand, but scaling typically requires both hands. In this work, we leverage the spatial alignment between gaze and hand as a mode switch to enable single-handed pinch-to-scale. We design and evaluate several techniques geared for one-handed scaling and assess their usability in a compound translate-scale task. Our findings show that all proposed methods effectively enable one-handed scaling, but each method offers distinct advantages and trade-offs. To this end, we derive design guidelines to support futuristic 3D interfaces with unimanual interaction. Our work helps make eye-hand 3D interaction in XR more mobile, flexible, and accessible.

</details>


### [113] [Cognitive Amplification vs Cognitive Delegation in Human-AI Systems: A Metric Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18677)
*Eduardo Di Santi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出区分AI增强人类认知与AI替代人类认知的框架，定义量化指标评估人机协同系统的认知可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI越来越多地嵌入人类决策过程，可能增强人类推理能力，也可能导致过度认知依赖。需要区分真正的认知增强与认知外包，确保人机协同系统的长期可持续性。

Method: 建立概念和数学框架，定义四个操作指标：认知放大指数(CAI*)、依赖比率(D)、人类依赖指数(HRI)和人类认知漂移率(HCDR)。这些指标构成低维度量空间，用于评估人机系统的协同性能和人类认知的可持续性。

Result: 框架揭示了人机系统设计中的核心矛盾：最大化短期混合能力不一定能保持长期人类认知能力。提出了认知可持续性约束的设计原则。

Conclusion: 人机系统应在认知可持续性约束下设计，确保混合性能的提升不以人类专业知识的退化为代价。需要平衡短期效率与长期人类认知能力的保持。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly embedded in human decision-making, where it can either enhance human reasoning or induce excessive cognitive dependence. This paper introduces a conceptual and mathematical framework for distinguishing cognitive amplification, in which AI improves hybrid human-AI performance while preserving human expertise, from cognitive delegation, in which reasoning is progressively outsourced to AI systems.
  To characterize these regimes, we define a set of operational metrics: the Cognitive Amplification Index (CAI*), the Dependency Ratio (D), the Human Reliance Index (HRI), and the Human Cognitive Drift Rate (HCDR). Together, these quantities provide a low-dimensional metric space for evaluating not only whether human-AI systems achieve genuine synergistic performance, but also whether such performance is cognitively sustainable for the human component over time.
  The framework highlights a central design tension in human-AI systems: maximizing short-term hybrid capability does not necessarily preserve long-term human cognitive competence. We therefore argue that human-AI systems should be designed under a cognitive sustainability constraint, such that gains in hybrid performance do not come at the cost of degradation in human expertise.

</details>


### [114] [Dual-Model Prediction of Affective Engagement and Vocal Attractiveness from Speaker Expressiveness in Video Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18758)
*Hung-Yue Suen,Kuo-En Hung,Fan-Hsun Tseng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习、仅使用演讲者侧情感表达的说话者中心情感AI方法，用于预测异步视频学习中观众的情感投入和声音吸引力，在独立测试集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受对可扩展、保护隐私的情感计算应用需求启发，开发一种仅依赖演讲者侧情感表达就能预测观众反馈的方法，避免收集观众侧信息带来的隐私和可扩展性问题。

Method: 采用说话者中心情感AI方法，包含两个独立的回归模型：1) 预测情感投入的模型，融合面部动态、眼动特征、韵律和认知语义等多模态特征；2) 预测声音吸引力的模型，仅基于演讲者侧声学特征。模型基于大规模在线开放课程(MOOCs)语料库开发。

Result: 在说话者独立的测试集上，两个回归模型均表现出优异的预测性能：情感投入预测的R²=0.85，声音吸引力预测的R²=0.88，表明仅凭演讲者侧情感特征就能有效代表聚合的观众反馈。

Conclusion: 本文通过实证研究证实，仅使用演讲者侧的多模态特征（包括声学特征）就能前瞻性地预测观众反馈，无需依赖观众侧输入信息，为可扩展、保护隐私的情感计算应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper outlines a machine learning-enabled speaker-centric Emotion AI approach capable of predicting audience-affective engagement and vocal attractiveness in asynchronous video-based learning, relying solely on speaker-side affective expressions. Inspired by the demand for scalable, privacy-preserving affective computing applications, this speaker-centric Emotion AI approach incorporates two distinct regression models that leverage a massive corpus developed within Massive Open Online Courses (MOOCs) to enable affectively engaging experiences. The regression model predicting affective engagement is developed by assimilating emotional expressions emanating from facial dynamics, oculomotor features, prosody, and cognitive semantics, while incorporating a second regression model to predict vocal attractiveness based exclusively on speaker-side acoustic features. Notably, on speaker-independent test sets, both regression models yielded impressive predictive performance (R2 = 0.85 for affective engagement and R2 = 0.88 for vocal attractiveness), confirming that speaker-side affect can functionally represent aggregated audience feedback. This paper provides a speaker-centric Emotion AI approach substantiated by an empirical study discovering that speaker-side multimodal features, including acoustics, can prospectively forecast audience feedback without necessarily employing audience-side input information.

</details>


### [115] [Through the Looking-Glass: AI-Mediated Video Communication Reduces Interpersonal Trust and Confidence in Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18868)
*Nelson Navajas Fernández,Jeffrey T. Hancock,Maurice Jakesch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI视频中介工具（美颜、背景替换、虚拟形象）不影响人们辨别谎言的能力，但会降低感知信任和判断信心


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI视频中介工具（如美颜、背景替换、虚拟形象）是否会影响人际信任、谎言检测能力和判断信心，以回应人们对AI可能破坏信任和可信度评估的担忧

Method: 采用两个预注册在线实验（N=2000），参与者观看说话者做出真实或欺骗性陈述的短视频，设置三个不同AI中介水平的条件：AI美颜、背景替换和虚拟形象，测量感知信任、谎言检测准确性和判断信心

Result: AI中介视频降低了感知信任和判断信心（尤其在虚拟形象使用不一致时），但实际谎言检测准确性未受影响，参与者不会更怀疑使用AI工具者说谎

Conclusion: AI中介不会损害人们区分真假的能力，反驳了基于线索的谎言检测理论，强调在信任和信心重要的情境中需要可信的AI中介工具

Abstract: AI-based tools that mediate, enhance or generate parts of video communication may interfere with how people evaluate trustworthiness and credibility. In two preregistered online experiments (N = 2,000), we examined whether AI-mediated video retouching, background replacement and avatars affect interpersonal trust, people's ability to detect lies and confidence in their judgments. Participants watched short videos of speakers making truthful or deceptive statements across three conditions with varying levels of AI mediation. We observed that perceived trust and confidence in judgments declined in AI-mediated videos, particularly in settings in which some participants used avatars while others did not. However, participants' actual judgment accuracy remained unchanged, and they were no more inclined to suspect those using AI tools of lying. Our findings provide evidence against concerns that AI mediation undermines people's ability to distinguish truth from lies, and against cue-based accounts of lie detection more generally. They highlight the importance of trustworthy AI mediation tools in contexts where not only truth, but also trust and confidence matter.

</details>


### [116] [From Accuracy to Readiness: Metrics and Benchmarks for Human-AI Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18895)
*Min Hun Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出评估人机决策的团队准备度框架，通过交互痕迹而非模型属性或自我报告信任度来评估校准、错误恢复和治理，支持更可比较的基准测试和累积研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统作为人类决策的协作者被部署，但评估实践主要关注模型准确性，而非人机团队是否准备好安全有效地协作。经验证据表明许多失败源于校准不当的依赖，包括AI错误时的过度使用和AI有帮助时的使用不足。

Method: 提出以团队准备度为中心的人机决策评估测量框架，引入包含结果、依赖行为、安全信号和时间学习四个部分的评估指标分类法，并将这些指标连接到人机入职和协作的"理解-控制-改进"生命周期。

Result: 通过交互痕迹而非模型属性或自我报告信任来操作化评估，使框架能够进行部署相关的校准、错误恢复和治理评估，支持更可比较的基准测试和人机准备度的累积研究。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在推进更安全、更负责任的人机协作，通过提供系统化的评估方法来解决当前人机协作评估中的关键缺陷，促进人机准备度的可比基准和累积研究。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems are deployed as collaborators in human decision-making. Yet, evaluation practices focus primarily on model accuracy rather than whether human-AI teams are prepared to collaborate safely and effectively. Empirical evidence shows that many failures arise from miscalibrated reliance, including overuse when AI is wrong and underuse when it is helpful.
  This paper proposes a measurement framework for evaluating human-AI decision-making centered on team readiness.
